    },
    {
        "name": "Copper",
        "kind": "chemical",
        "answer": "Cu",
        "alias": "Cuprum",
        "clue_train": "The reddish metal commonly used in electrical wiring",
        "clue_test": "Which reddish conductive metal is used in wires",
        "reverse_train": "Cu is the chemical symbol of which element?",
        "reverse_test": "The symbol Cu represents which chemical element?",
        "distractor_train": "The chemical symbol for copper is not Fe, it is",
        "distractor_test": "Despite iron being Fe, the symbol for copper is",
    },
]

CONCEPT_NAMES = [c["name"] for c in CONCEPTS]
CONCEPT_TO_IDX = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(CONCEPT_NAMES)}
N_CONCEPTS = len(CONCEPTS)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Build train/test prompt rows
# ------------------------------------------------------------
train_rows = []
test_rows = []


def add_row(rows, prompt, concept, typ, split, target_text, mask_terms):
    rows.append({
        "prompt": prompt,
        "concept": concept,
        "type": typ,
        "split": split,
        "target_text": target_text,
        "mask_terms": mask_terms,
    })


for c in CONCEPTS:
    name = c["name"]
    kind = c["kind"]
    answer = c["answer"]
    alias = c["alias"]

    if kind == "country":
        direct_train = [
            f"The capital of {name} is",
            f"{name}'s capital is",
            f"Please name the capital of {name}:",
        ]
        direct_test = [
            f"What is the capital of {name}?",
        ]

        alias_train = [
            f"The capital of {alias} is",
        ]
        alias_test = [
            f"Tell me the capital of {alias}.",
        ]

        clue_train = [
            f"{c['clue_train']}, its capital is",
        ]
        clue_test = [
            f"{c['clue_test']}, its capital is",
        ]

        reverse_train = [
            c["reverse_train"],
        ]
        reverse_test = [
            c["reverse_test"],
        ]

        distractor_train = [
            c["distractor_train"],
        ]
        distractor_test = [
            c["distractor_test"],
        ]

        # For reverse query, expected output is concept name, not answer.
        for p in direct_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "direct", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in direct_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "direct", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in alias_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "alias", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in alias_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "alias", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in clue_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "clue", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in clue_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "clue", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in reverse_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "reverse", "train", name, [name])
        for p in reverse_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "reverse", "test", name, [name])

        for p in distractor_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "distractor", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in distractor_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "distractor", "test", answer, [name])

    else:
        direct_train = [
            f"The chemical symbol for {name} is",
            f"{name} has the chemical symbol",
            f"Please give the chemical symbol for {name}:",
        ]
        direct_test = [
            f"What is the chemical symbol for {name}?",
        ]

        alias_train = [
            f"The chemical symbol for {alias} is",
        ]
        alias_test = [
            f"State the symbol for {alias}.",
        ]

        clue_train = [
            f"{c['clue_train']}, its symbol is",
        ]
        clue_test = [
            f"{c['clue_test']}, its symbol is",
        ]

        reverse_train = [
            c["reverse_train"],
        ]
        reverse_test = [
            c["reverse_test"],
        ]

        distractor_train = [
            c["distractor_train"],
        ]
        distractor_test = [
            c["distractor_test"],
        ]

        for p in direct_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "direct", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in direct_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "direct", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in alias_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "alias", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in alias_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "alias", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in clue_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "clue", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in clue_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "clue", "test", answer, [name])

        for p in reverse_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "reverse", "train", name, [name])
        for p in reverse_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "reverse", "test", name, [name])

        for p in distractor_train:
            add_row(train_rows, p, name, "distractor", "train", answer, [name])
        for p in distractor_test:
            add_row(test_rows, p, name, "distractor", "test", answer, [name])


all_rows = train_rows + test_rows

print(f"🧠 Train prompts: {len(train_rows)}")
print(f"🧪 Test paraphrase prompts: {len(test_rows)}")
print(f"🧩 Concepts stored in DNA: {N_CONCEPTS}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. DNA-owned hash encoder + masking
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def char_hash_features(text, dim=HASH_DIM):
    text = text.lower()
    feats = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float32)

    def add(gram):
        h = int(hashlib.md5(gram.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(), 16)
        idx = h % dim
        sign = 1.0 if ((h // dim) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
        feats[idx] += sign

    for n in (2, 3, 4):
        for i in range(max(0, len(text) - n + 1)):
            add(text[i:i + n])

    clean = (
        text.replace("'", " ")
            .replace(",", " ")
            .replace(":", " ")
            .replace("?", " ")
            .replace("!", " ")
            .replace(".", " ")
    )
    for w in clean.split():
        if len(w) > 1:
            add("W" + w)
            if len(w) > 3:
                add("P" + w[:3])
                add("S" + w[-3:])

    norm = np.linalg.norm(feats)
    if norm > 1e-8:
        feats = feats / norm

    return torch.from_numpy(feats)


def mask_text(text, terms):
    t = text.lower()
    for term in sorted(terms, key=len, reverse=True):
        t = t.replace(term.lower(), " ")
    return t


for r in all_rows:
    r["hash_raw"] = char_hash_features(r["prompt"])
    r["hash_masked"] = char_hash_features(mask_text(r["prompt"], r["mask_terms"]))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Load teacher model for extraction only
# ------------------------------------------------------------
try:
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL,
        dtype=torch.float32,
        low_cpu_mem_usage=True
    )
except TypeError:
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL,
        torch_dtype=torch.float32,
        low_cpu_mem_usage=True
    )

tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL)
tok.padding_side = "left"
if tok.pad_token is None:
    tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token

model.eval()

NL = model.config.num_hidden_layers
D = model.config.hidden_size

embed_layer = model.get_input_embeddings()
W_unembed = model.get_output_embeddings().weight.detach().float()

print(f"✅ Teacher loaded for extraction | layers={NL} | hidden={D} | vocab={W_unembed.shape[0]}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Capture teacher 8-lens features
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def capture_teacher_rows(rows, batch_size=4):
    for start in range(0, len(rows), batch_size):
        batch = rows[start:start + batch_size]
        prompts = [r["prompt"] for r in batch]

        inp = tok(
            prompts,
            return_tensors="pt",
            padding=True,
            truncation=True,
            max_length=40
        )
        mask = inp["attention_mask"].bool()

        with torch.inference_mode():
            out = model(**inp, output_hidden_states=True)
            emb = embed_layer(inp["input_ids"]).float()

        logits = out.logits[:, -1, :].float()
        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)

        topvals, _ = probs.topk(5, dim=-1)
        top1_prob = topvals[:, 0]
        margin = topvals[:, 0] - topvals[:, 1]

        p = probs.clamp_min(1e-12)
        entropy = -(p * p.log()).sum(dim=-1)

        hs = out.hidden_states

        for b, r in enumerate(batch):
            # Target token id for behavior expected probability
            target_ids = tok(r["target_text"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
            target_id = target_ids[0] if target_ids else tok("<unk>").input_ids[0]
            r["target_id"] = int(target_id)
            expected_prob = probs[b, target_id]

            # Lens 3: activation wave, last-token hidden state per layer
            H = torch.stack(
                [hs[l + 1][b, -1, :].float() for l in range(NL)],
                dim=0
            )  # (NL, D)

            # Lens 4: weight/activity pointer proxy = layer delta norms
            deltas = torch.stack(
                [hs[l + 1][b, -1, :].float() - hs[l][b, -1, :].float() for l in range(NL)],
                dim=0
            )
            delta_norms = deltas.norm(dim=1)

            # Lens 1: ant-bil routing flow = cosine between consecutive layers
            if NL > 1:
                flow = F.cosine_similarity(H[:-1], H[1:], dim=1)
            else:
                flow = torch.zeros(0, dtype=torch.float32)

            # Lens 2/7: behavior/logit stats
            beh = torch.stack([
                top1_prob[b],
                margin[b],
                entropy[b],
                expected_prob
            ]).float()

            r["H"] = H.reshape(-1).cpu()
            r["delta_norms"] = delta_norms.cpu()
            r["flow"] = flow.cpu()
            r["beh"] = beh.cpu()


print("\n📐 Capturing teacher 8-lens features...")
capture_teacher_rows(all_rows)
print(f"✅ Capture done ({time.time() - t0:.1f}s)")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Build shared knowledge wave basis from TRAIN rows only
# ------------------------------------------------------------
Xtrain = torch.stack([r["H"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)  # (n_train, NL*D)
mu = Xtrain.mean(dim=0)
Xc = Xtrain - mu

M = Xc.shape[1]

with torch.inference_mode():
    _, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(Xc.double(), full_matrices=False)

if S.numel() > 0 and float(S[0]) > 0:
    tol = max(Xc.shape) * float(S[0]) * 1e-12
    eff_rank = int((S > tol).sum().item())
else:
    eff_rank = 0

R = min(8, max(1, eff_rank))
V = Vh[:R].float()
V_final = V[:, (NL - 1) * D: NL * D].contiguous()

print(f"✅ Wave basis built | response_dim={M} | effective_rank={eff_rank} | used_rank={R}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Derive per-row lens features: amp, sens, route, beh
# ------------------------------------------------------------
for r in all_rows:
    # Lens 8: knowledge wave amplitudes
    r["amp"] = ((r["H"] - mu) @ V.T).float().cpu()

    # Lens 6: output sensitivity of expected target token in final-layer wave space
    w_target = W_unembed[r["target_id"]].float()
    r["sens"] = (V_final @ w_target).float().cpu()

    # Lens 1+4: routing/pointer vector
    r["route"] = torch.cat([r["delta_norms"], r["flow"]], dim=0).float().cpu()

    # Lens 2+7: behavior vector already r["beh"]


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Build DNA atoms from TRAIN rows
# ------------------------------------------------------------
atoms = []

for c in CONCEPTS:
    name = c["name"]
    rs = [r for r in train_rows if r["concept"] == name]

    atom = {
        "concept": name,
        "answer": c["answer"],
        "idx": CONCEPT_TO_IDX[name],
        "amp": torch.stack([r["amp"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
        "sens": torch.stack([r["sens"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
        "route": torch.stack([r["route"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
        "beh": torch.stack([r["beh"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
        "hash_raw": torch.stack([r["hash_raw"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
        "hash_masked": torch.stack([r["hash_masked"] for r in rs], dim=0).mean(dim=0),
    }

    atoms.append(atom)

print(f"✅ DNA atoms built: {len(atoms)} concepts")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Train DNA-only routers: hash -> lens targets
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def ridge_fit(X, Y, lam=1e-2):
    X = X.double()
    Y = Y.double()

    bias = torch.ones(X.shape[0], 1, dtype=X.dtype)
    Xa = torch.cat([X, bias], dim=1)

    A = Xa.T @ Xa
    A = A + lam * torch.eye(A.shape[0], dtype=X.dtype)

    B = Xa.T @ Y

    try:
        W = torch.linalg.solve(A, B)
    except Exception:
        W = torch.linalg.lstsq(A, B).solution

    return W.float()


def ridge_predict(X, W):
    X = X.double()
    bias = torch.ones(X.shape[0], 1, dtype=X.dtype)
    Xa = torch.cat([X, bias], dim=1)
    return (Xa @ W.double()).float()


Xraw_train = torch.stack([r["hash_raw"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)
Xmask_train = torch.stack([r["hash_masked"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)

Yamp_train = torch.stack([r["amp"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)
Ysens_train = torch.stack([r["sens"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)
Yroute_train = torch.stack([r["route"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)
Ybeh_train = torch.stack([r["beh"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)

W_amp_raw = ridge_fit(Xraw_train, Yamp_train, lam=1e-2)
W_amp_mask = ridge_fit(Xmask_train, Yamp_train, lam=1e-2)

W_sens_raw = ridge_fit(Xraw_train, Ysens_train, lam=1e-2)
W_sens_mask = ridge_fit(Xmask_train, Ysens_train, lam=1e-2)

W_route_raw = ridge_fit(Xraw_train, Yroute_train, lam=1e-2)
W_route_mask = ridge_fit(Xmask_train, Yroute_train, lam=1e-2)

W_beh_raw = ridge_fit(Xraw_train, Ybeh_train, lam=1e-2)
W_beh_mask = ridge_fit(Xmask_train, Ybeh_train, lam=1e-2)

print("✅ DNA-only routers trained")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Delete teacher model. From here inference is DNA-only.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n🧹 Deleting teacher model. Inference will use DNA arrays only...")

del model
del embed_layer
del W_unembed
del tok
gc.collect()

print(f"✅ Teacher deleted. Memory freed. ({time.time() - t0:.1f}s)")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. Lens scoring helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
LENS_NAMES = [
    "raw_hash",
    "masked_hash",
    "wave_raw",
    "wave_masked",
    "sens_raw",
    "sens_masked",
    "route_raw",
    "route_masked",
    "beh_raw",
    "beh_masked",
]
N_LENS = len(LENS_NAMES)


def unit(v):
    v = v.float()
    return v / v.norm().clamp(min=1e-8)


def cos_1d(a, b):
    return float((unit(a) * unit(b)).sum().item())


def compute_row_lens(row):
    hraw = row["hash_raw"].float()
    hmask = row["hash_masked"].float()

    pred_amp_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_amp_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_sens_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_sens_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_sens_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_sens_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_route_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_route_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_route_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_route_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_beh_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_beh_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_beh_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_beh_mask).squeeze(0)

    lens_scores = torch.zeros(len(atoms), N_LENS, dtype=torch.float32)

    for ai, atom in enumerate(atoms):
        lens_scores[ai, 0] = cos_1d(hraw, atom["hash_raw"])
        lens_scores[ai, 1] = cos_1d(hmask, atom["hash_masked"])
        lens_scores[ai, 2] = cos_1d(pred_amp_raw, atom["amp"])
        lens_scores[ai, 3] = cos_1d(pred_amp_mask, atom["amp"])
        lens_scores[ai, 4] = cos_1d(pred_sens_raw, atom["sens"])
        lens_scores[ai, 5] = cos_1d(pred_sens_mask, atom["sens"])
        lens_scores[ai, 6] = cos_1d(pred_route_raw, atom["route"])
        lens_scores[ai, 7] = cos_1d(pred_route_mask, atom["route"])
        lens_scores[ai, 8] = cos_1d(pred_beh_raw, atom["beh"])
        lens_scores[ai, 9] = cos_1d(pred_beh_mask, atom["beh"])

    row["lens_scores"] = lens_scores
    row["feat_full"] = lens_scores.reshape(-1)


for r in all_rows:
    compute_row_lens(r)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. Train learned fusion classifiers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def one_hot(indices, n_classes):
    Y = torch.zeros(len(indices), n_classes, dtype=torch.float32)
    Y[torch.arange(len(indices)), indices] = 1.0
    return Y


train_indices = [CONCEPT_TO_IDX[r["concept"]] for r in train_rows]
Yfusion = one_hot(train_indices, N_CONCEPTS)
Xfusion = torch.stack([r["feat_full"] for r in train_rows], dim=0)


def subset_features(feat, lens_keep):
    # feat shape: n_atoms * N_LENS
    ls = feat.view(len(atoms), N_LENS)[:, lens_keep]
    return ls.reshape(-1)


FUSION_SUBSETS = {
    "fusion_all": list(range(N_LENS)),
    "fusion_no_raw": list(range(1, N_LENS)),
    "fusion_semantic_only": list(range(2, N_LENS)),
}

W_fusion = {}

for name, lenses in FUSION_SUBSETS.items():
    Xsub = torch.stack([subset_features(r["feat_full"], lenses) for r in train_rows], dim=0)
    W_fusion[name] = ridge_fit(Xsub, Yfusion, lam=1.0)

print("✅ Learned fusion classifiers trained")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 13. Prediction modes
# ------------------------------------------------------------
SIMILARITY_MODES = {
    "raw_hash": 0,
    "masked_hash": 1,
    "wave_raw": 2,
    "wave_masked": 3,
    "sens_raw": 4,
    "sens_masked": 5,
    "route_raw": 6,
    "route_masked": 7,
    "beh_raw": 8,
    "beh_masked": 9,
}


def predict_similarity(row, lens_idx):
    scores = row["lens_scores"][:, lens_idx]
    best = int(torch.argmax(scores).item())
    return atoms[best]["concept"], scores


def predict_fusion(row, fusion_name):
    lenses = FUSION_SUBSETS[fusion_name]
    x = subset_features(row["feat_full"], lenses).unsqueeze(0)
    logits = ridge_predict(x, W_fusion[fusion_name]).squeeze(0)
    best = int(torch.argmax(logits).item())
    return atoms[best]["concept"], logits


def display_target_for_prompt(row, pred_concept):
    if row["type"] == "reverse":
        return pred_concept
    # find answer
    for c in CONCEPTS:
        if c["name"] == pred_concept:
            return c["answer"]
    return "?"


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 1 + 2 + 3: Mode accuracy by prompt type
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 TEST 1 + 2 + 3: DNA-ONLY MODE ACCURACY BY PROMPT TYPE")
print("=" * 104)

TYPE_ORDER = ["direct", "alias", "clue", "reverse", "distractor"]

MODE_LIST = []
for k in SIMILARITY_MODES.keys():
    MODE_LIST.append(k)
for k in FUSION_SUBSETS.keys():
    MODE_LIST.append(k)

print(f"{'Mode':<22}{'Overall%':<11}{'Direct%':<10}{'Alias%':<10}{'Clue%':<10}{'Reverse%':<11}{'Distractor%'}")
print("-" * 104)

mode_results = {}

for mode in MODE_LIST:
    type_correct = {t: 0 for t in TYPE_ORDER}
    type_total = {t: 0 for t in TYPE_ORDER}
    overall_correct = 0

    for r in test_rows:
        if mode in SIMILARITY_MODES:
            pred, _ = predict_similarity(r, SIMILARITY_MODES[mode])
        else:
            pred, _ = predict_fusion(r, mode)

        typ = r["type"]
        type_total[typ] += 1

        if pred == r["concept"]:
            overall_correct += 1
            type_correct[typ] += 1

    overall_acc = overall_correct / max(1, len(test_rows))
    type_acc = {
        t: type_correct[t] / max(1, type_total[t])
        for t in TYPE_ORDER
    }

    mode_results[mode] = {
        "overall": overall_acc,
        "types": type_acc,
    }

    print(
        f"{mode:<22}{overall_acc * 100:<11.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['direct'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['alias'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['clue'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['reverse'] * 100:<11.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['distractor'] * 100:.1f}"
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 4: Detailed predictions for important modes
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 TEST 4: DETAILED PREDICTIONS — fusion_no_raw")
print("=" * 104)

print(f"{'Type':<12}{'Prompt':<58}{'True':<12}{'Pred':<12}{'Display':<16}{'OK'}")
print("-" * 122)

fusion_no_raw_correct = 0

for r in test_rows:
    pred, logits = predict_fusion(r, "fusion_no_raw")
    disp = display_target_for_prompt(r, pred)
    ok = (pred == r["concept"])
    if ok:
        fusion_no_raw_correct += 1

    short_p = r["prompt"] if len(r["prompt"]) <= 56 else r["prompt"][:53] + "..."
    mark = "🟢" if ok else "🔴"

    print(
        f"{r['type']:<12}{short_p:<58}{r['concept']:<12}{pred:<12}{disp:<16}{mark}"
    )

fusion_no_raw_acc = fusion_no_raw_correct / max(1, len(test_rows))
print(f"\nfusion_no_raw overall accuracy: {fusion_no_raw_acc * 100:.1f}%")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 5: Discriminative margin by prompt type
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 TEST 5: DISCRIMINATIVE MARGIN — fusion_no_raw")
print("   Margin = top logit - second logit")
print("=" * 104)

margin_by_type = {t: [] for t in TYPE_ORDER}

for r in test_rows:
    _, logits = predict_fusion(r, "fusion_no_raw")
    top2 = torch.topk(logits, 2)
    margin = float((top2.values[0] - top2.values[1]).item())
    margin_by_type[r["type"]].append(margin)

print(f"{'Type':<14}{'AvgMargin':<12}{'MinMargin':<12}{'MaxMargin':<12}{'Verdict'}")
print("-" * 78)

for t in TYPE_ORDER:
    vals = margin_by_type[t]
    if not vals:
        continue
    avg = float(np.mean(vals))
    mn = float(np.min(vals))
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    verdict = "🟢" if avg >= 0.20 else ("🟡" if avg >= 0.08 else "🔴")
    print(f"{t:<14}{avg:<12.4f}{mn:<12.4f}{mx:<12.4f}{verdict}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 6: Unknown concept rejection diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 TEST 6: UNKNOWN CONCEPT REJECTION DIAGNOSTIC")
print("=" * 104)

UNKNOWN_PROMPTS = [
    "The capital of Atlantis is",
    "The capital of Nowhereland is",
    "The chemical symbol for Unobtainium is",
    "The chemical symbol for Fictionium is",
    "The capital of Moonville is",
]

print(f"{'Prompt':<48}{'TopPred':<14}{'RawMargin':<12}{'FusionMargin':<15}{'Status'}")
print("-" * 108)

for p in UNKNOWN_PROMPTS:
    row = {
        "prompt": p,
        "concept": None,
        "type": "unknown",
        "split": "unknown",
        "target_text": "",
        "mask_terms": [],
    }
    row["hash_raw"] = char_hash_features(p)
    row["hash_masked"] = char_hash_features(mask_text(p, []))
    compute_row_lens(row)

    # raw hash margin
    raw_scores = row["lens_scores"][:, 0]
    raw_top2 = torch.topk(raw_scores, 2)
    raw_margin = float((raw_top2.values[0] - raw_top2.values[1]).item())
    raw_pred = atoms[int(raw_top2.indices[0].item())]["concept"]

    # fusion_no_raw probability margin
    pred_fusion, logits = predict_fusion(row, "fusion_no_raw")
    probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
    top2p = torch.topk(probs, 2)
    fusion_margin = float((top2p.values[0] - top2p.values[1]).item())

    if fusion_margin < 0.05:
        status = "🟡 LOW CONFIDENCE"
    elif fusion_margin < 0.15:
        status = "🟠 WEAK CONFIDENCE"
    else:
        status = "🔴 OVERCONFIDENT"

    short_p = p if len(p) <= 46 else p[:43] + "..."
    print(
        f"{short_p:<48}{pred_fusion:<14}{raw_margin:<12.4f}{fusion_margin:<15.4f}{status}"
    )

print("\nNote: Unknown concepts are not in DNA memory. Good DNA should show low margin.")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 7: 1MB DNA size budget
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 TEST 7: 1MB DNA SIZE BUDGET")
print("=" * 104)

route_dim = NL + max(0, NL - 1)
beh_dim = 4

basis_params = R * M + M

router_params = 2 * (HASH_DIM + 1) * (R + R + route_dim + beh_dim)

atom_params = N_CONCEPTS * (
    R + R + route_dim + beh_dim + 2 * HASH_DIM
)

answer_text_chars = sum(len(c["answer"]) + len(c["name"]) for c in CONCEPTS)
answer_text_params = answer_text_chars  # rough 1 byte/char if stored as ASCII/UTF-8 compact

fusion_features_all = N_CONCEPTS * N_LENS
fusion_params = (fusion_features_all + 1) * N_CONCEPTS

meta_params = 128

total_params = (
    basis_params +
    router_params +
    atom_params +
    answer_text_params +
    fusion_params +
    meta_params
)

fp16_bytes = total_params * 2
fp16_mb = fp16_bytes / 1_000_000.0

print(f"Used wave rank                 : {R}")
print(f"Response wave dimension        : {M}")
print(f"Number of DNA atoms            : {N_CONCEPTS}")
print(f"Hash encoder dim               : {HASH_DIM}")
print(f"Route feature dim              : {route_dim}")
print(f"Behavior feature dim           : {beh_dim}")
print()
print(f"Wave basis params              : {basis_params:,}")
print(f"Router params                  : {router_params:,}")
print(f"Atom params                    : {atom_params:,}")
print(f"Answer text params (rough)     : {answer_text_params:,}")
print(f"Fusion classifier params       : {fusion_params:,}")
print(f"Meta params                    : {meta_params:,}")
print(f"Total params                   : {total_params:,}")
print(f"fp16 estimated size            : {fp16_mb:.3f} MB")

if fp16_mb <= 1.0:
    print("✅ This PD-4 anti-shortcut DNA prototype fits under 1MB.")
else:
    print("🔴 Current prototype exceeds 1MB; reduce rank/hash dim/features.")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# FINAL VERDICT
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🏆 PD-4 FINAL VERDICT")
print("=" * 104)

best_mode = max(mode_results.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["overall"])
best_name, best_res = best_mode

raw_hash_res = mode_results["raw_hash"]
masked_hash_res = mode_results["masked_hash"]
fusion_no_raw_res = mode_results["fusion_no_raw"]
fusion_semantic_res = mode_results["fusion_semantic_only"]

print(f"1. Best overall mode                 : {best_name} ({best_res['overall'] * 100:.1f}%)")
print(f"2. raw_hash overall                  : {raw_hash_res['overall'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"3. masked_hash overall               : {masked_hash_res['overall'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"4. fusion_no_raw overall             : {fusion_no_raw_res['overall'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"5. fusion_semantic_only overall      : {fusion_semantic_res['overall'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"6. fusion_no_raw clue accuracy       : {fusion_no_raw_res['types']['clue'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"7. fusion_no_raw reverse accuracy    : {fusion_no_raw_res['types']['reverse'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"8. fusion_no_raw distractor accuracy : {fusion_no_raw_res['types']['distractor'] * 100:.1f}%")
print(f"9. DNA prototype size                : {fp16_mb:.3f} MB")
print()

shortcut_gap = raw_hash_res["overall"] - masked_hash_res["overall"]

if shortcut_gap > 0.20:
    print("🟡 LEXICAL SHORTCUT DETECTED:")
    print("   raw_hash is much stronger than masked_hash.")
    print("   Matlab DNA ka kuch hissa directly entity naam pe depend kar raha hai.")
else:
    print("🟢 LEXICAL SHORTCUT NOT DOMINANT:")
    print("   masked_hash ne raw_hash ke kareeb performance di.")
    print("   Matlab sirf entity naam match se test pass nahi ho raha.")

if fusion_no_raw_res["types"]["clue"] >= 0.70:
    print("✅ SUPPORTED: fusion_no_raw retrieves stored concepts from clue prompts.")
else:
    print("🟡 PARTIAL: clue-based retrieval needs stronger wave/router features.")

if fusion_no_raw_res["types"]["reverse"] >= 0.70:
    print("✅ SUPPORTED: reverse queries work, so DNA stores concept-answer bidirectional associations.")
else:
    print("🟡 PARTIAL: reverse query retrieval is weak.")

if fusion_no_raw_res["types"]["distractor"] >= 0.70:
    print("✅ SUPPORTED: distractor prompts are handled reasonably.")
else:
    print("🟡 PARTIAL: distractor robustness needs improvement.")

if fusion_semantic_res["overall"] >= 0.70:
    print("✅ STRONG: semantic-only fusion (no hash lenses) works well.")
    print("   This is the best evidence that DNA is not just keyword lookup.")
elif fusion_semantic_res["overall"] >= 0.50:
    print("🟡 USABLE: semantic-only fusion works partially.")
else:
    print("🔴 WEAK: semantic-only fusion is not reliable yet.")

if fp16_mb <= 1.0:
    print("✅ SIZE: narrow-domain anti-shortcut DNA cartridge can fit under 1MB.")
else:
    print("🔴 SIZE: needs compression to meet 1MB target.")

print()

# ============================================================
# 🧬 PD-5: BEST ANTI-SHORTCUT DNA — ONE CELL CPU PROTOTYPE
# ============================================================
# Goal:
# Teacher model ki knowledge ko DNA atoms mein extract karo.
# Teacher delete karo.
# DNA-only inference se:
#   direct / alias / clue / reverse / distractor prompts par
#   stored concepts retrieve karo.
#
# Key upgrades over PD-4:
# 1. Per-domain wave basis:
#    - country basis rank 6
#    - chemical basis rank 10
# 2. Reverse prompts included in calibration/training.
# 3. Stronger distinct chemical clues.
# 4. Raw + masked semantic routers.
# 5. Learned fusion over lenses.
# 6. Unknown concept rejection margin.
# 7. 1MB size budget.
#
# Honest limit:
# This tests concepts STORED in DNA memory.
# It does NOT prove full general LLM replacement.
# ============================================================

CONCEPT_NAMES = [c["name"] for c in CONCEPTS]
CONCEPT_TO_IDX = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(CONCEPT_NAMES)}
CONCEPT_KIND = {c["name"]: c["kind"] for c in CONCEPTS}
ANSWER_MAP = {c["name"]: c["answer"] for c in CONCEPTS}
N_CONCEPTS = len(CONCEPTS)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Build train/test rows
# ------------------------------------------------------------
train_rows = []
test_rows = []


def add_row(rows, prompt, concept, typ, split, target_text, mask_terms):
    rows.append({
        "prompt": prompt,
        "concept": concept,
        "type": typ,
        "split": split,
        "target_text": target_text,
        "mask_terms": mask_terms,
    })


for c in CONCEPTS:
    name = c["name"]
    kind = c["kind"]
    answer = c["answer"]
    alias = c["alias"]

    if kind == "country":
        direct_train = [
            f"The capital of {name} is",
            f"{name}'s capital is",
            f"Please name the capital of {name}:",
        ]
        direct_test = [
            f"What is the capital of {name}?",
        ]

        alias_train = [
            f"The capital of {alias} is",
        ]
        alias_test = [
            f"Tell me the capital of {alias}.",
        ]

        clue_train = [
            f"{c['clue_train']}, its capital is",
        ]
        clue_test = [
            f"{c['clue_test']}, its capital is",
        ]

        reverse_train = [
            c["reverse_train"],
        ]
        reverse_test = [
            c["reverse_test"],
        ]

        distractor_train = [
            c["distractor_train"],
        ]
        distractor_test = [
            c["distractor_test"],
        ]

        target_forward = answer
        target_reverse = name

    else:
        direct_train = [
            f"The chemical symbol for {name} is",
            f"{name} has the chemical symbol",
            f"Please give the chemical symbol for {name}:",
        ]
        direct_test = [
            f"What is the chemical symbol for {name}?",
        ]

        alias_train = [
            f"The chemical symbol for {alias} is",
        ]
        alias_test = [
            f"State the symbol for {alias}.",
        ]

        clue_train = [
            f"{c['clue_train']}, its symbol is",
        ]
        clue_test = [
            f"{c['clue_test']}, its symbol is",
        ]

        reverse_train = [
            c["reverse_train"],
        ]
        reverse_test = [
            c["reverse_test"],
        ]

        distractor_train = [
            c["distractor_train"],
        ]
        distractor_test = [
            c["distractor_test"],
        ]

        target_forward = answer
        target_reverse = name

    mask_terms = [name]

    for p in direct_train:
        add_row(train_rows, p, name, "direct", "train", target_forward, mask_terms)
    for p in direct_test:
        add_row(test_rows, p, name, "direct", "test", target_forward, mask_terms)

    for p in alias_train:
        add_row(train_rows, p, name, "alias", "train", target_forward, mask_terms)
    for p in alias_test:
        add_row(test_rows, p, name, "alias", "test", target_forward, mask_terms)

    for p in clue_train:
        add_row(train_rows, p, name, "clue", "train", target_forward, mask_terms)
    for p in clue_test:
        add_row(test_rows, p, name, "clue", "test", target_forward, mask_terms)

    for p in reverse_train:
        add_row(train_rows, p, name, "reverse", "train", target_reverse, mask_terms)
    for p in reverse_test:
        add_row(test_rows, p, name, "reverse", "test", target_reverse, mask_terms)

    for p in distractor_train:
        add_row(train_rows, p, name, "distractor", "train", target_forward, mask_terms)
    for p in distractor_test:
        add_row(test_rows, p, name, "distractor", "test", target_forward, mask_terms)

all_rows = train_rows + test_rows

print(f"🧠 Train prompts: {len(train_rows)}")
print(f"🧪 Test paraphrase prompts: {len(test_rows)}")
print(f"🧩 Concepts stored in DNA: {N_CONCEPTS}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. DNA-owned hash encoder + masking
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def char_hash_features(text, dim=HASH_DIM):
    text = text.lower()
    feats = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float32)

    def add(gram):
        h = int(hashlib.md5(gram.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(), 16)
        idx = h % dim
        sign = 1.0 if ((h // dim) & 1) == 0 else -1.0
        feats[idx] += sign

    for n in (2, 3, 4):
        for i in range(max(0, len(text) - n + 1)):
            add(text[i:i + n])

    clean = (
        text.replace("'", " ")
            .replace(",", " ")
            .replace(":", " ")
            .replace("?", " ")
            .replace("!", " ")
            .replace(".", " ")
    )
    for w in clean.split():
        if len(w) > 1:
            add("W" + w)
            if len(w) > 3:
                add("P" + w[:3])
                add("S" + w[-3:])

    norm = np.linalg.norm(feats)
    if norm > 1e-8:
        feats = feats / norm

    return torch.from_numpy(feats)


def mask_text(text, terms):
    t = text.lower()
    for term in sorted(terms, key=len, reverse=True):
        t = t.replace(term.lower(), " ")
    return t


for r in all_rows:
    r["hash_raw"] = char_hash_features(r["prompt"])
    r["hash_masked"] = char_hash_features(mask_text(r["prompt"], r["mask_terms"]))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Load teacher model for extraction only
# ------------------------------------------------------------
try:
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL,
        dtype=torch.float32,
        low_cpu_mem_usage=True
    )
except TypeError:
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL,
        torch_dtype=torch.float32,
        low_cpu_mem_usage=True
    )

tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL)
tok.padding_side = "left"
if tok.pad_token is None:
    tok.pad_token = tok.eos_token

model.eval()

NL = model.config.num_hidden_layers
D = model.config.hidden_size

W_unembed = model.get_output_embeddings().weight.detach().float()

print(f"✅ Teacher loaded for extraction | layers={NL} | hidden={D} | vocab={W_unembed.shape[0]}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Capture teacher activation waves
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def capture_teacher_rows(rows, batch_size=4):
    for start in range(0, len(rows), batch_size):
        batch = rows[start:start + batch_size]
        prompts = [r["prompt"] for r in batch]

        inp = tok(
            prompts,
            return_tensors="pt",
            padding=True,
            truncation=True,
            max_length=48
        )

        with torch.inference_mode():
            out = model(**inp, output_hidden_states=True)

        hs = out.hidden_states

        for b, r in enumerate(batch):
            target_ids = tok(r["target_text"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
            target_id = target_ids[0] if target_ids else tok("<unk>").input_ids[0]
            r["target_id"] = int(target_id)

            H = torch.stack(
                [hs[l + 1][b, -1, :].float() for l in range(NL)],
                dim=0
            )  # (NL, D)

            r["H"] = H.reshape(-1).cpu()


print("\n📐 Capturing teacher activation waves...")
capture_teacher_rows(all_rows)
print(f"✅ Capture done ({time.time() - t0:.1f}s)")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Build per-domain wave bases from TRAIN rows only
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def build_basis(rows, desired_rank, domain_name):
    if len(rows) == 0:
        return {
            "mu": torch.zeros(NL * D, dtype=torch.float32),
            "V": torch.zeros((1, NL * D), dtype=torch.float32),
            "V_final": torch.zeros((1, D), dtype=torch.float32),
            "R": 1,
        }

    X = torch.stack([r["H"] for r in rows], dim=0)
    mu = X.mean(dim=0)
    Xc = X - mu

    with torch.inference_mode():
        _, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(Xc.double(), full_matrices=False)

    if S.numel() > 0 and float(S[0]) > 0:
        tol = max(Xc.shape) * float(S[0]) * 1e-12
        eff_rank = int((S > tol).sum().item())
    else:
        eff_rank = 0

    R = min(desired_rank, max(1, eff_rank))
    V = Vh[:R].float()
    V_final = V[:, (NL - 1) * D: NL * D].contiguous()

    print(f"   {domain_name}: n={len(rows)} | effective_rank={eff_rank} | used_rank={R}")

    return {
        "mu": mu,
        "V": V,
        "V_final": V_final,
        "R": R,
    }


country_train = [r for r in train_rows if CONCEPT_KIND[r["concept"]] == "country"]
chemical_train = [r for r in train_rows if CONCEPT_KIND[r["concept"]] == "chemical"]

print("\n🧠 Building per-domain wave bases...")
basis_c = build_basis(country_train, R_COUNTRY_DESIRED, "country")
basis_k = build_basis(chemical_train, R_CHEMICAL_DESIRED, "chemical")

R_C = basis_c["R"]
R_K = basis_k["R"]
M = NL * D

print(f"✅ Wave bases ready | country_rank={R_C} | chemical_rank={R_K} | response_dim={M}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Compute true lens features for all rows
# ------------------------------------------------------------
for r in all_rows:
    H = r["H"]

    r["amp_c"] = ((H - basis_c["mu"]) @ basis_c["V"].T).float().cpu()
    r["amp_k"] = ((H - basis_k["mu"]) @ basis_k["V"].T).float().cpu()

    w_target = W_unembed[r["target_id"]].float()
    r["sens_c"] = (basis_c["V_final"] @ w_target).float().cpu()
    r["sens_k"] = (basis_k["V_final"] @ w_target).float().cpu()


# Free large teacher output embedding reference before deleting model.
del W_unembed


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Build DNA atoms from TRAIN rows
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def mean_or_zero(rows, key, dim):
    if len(rows) == 0:
        return torch.zeros(dim, dtype=torch.float32)
    return torch.stack([r[key] for r in rows], dim=0).mean(dim=0).float().cpu()


atoms = []

for c in CONCEPTS:
    name = c["name"]
    rs = [r for r in train_rows if r["concept"] == name]
    fwd = [r for r in rs if r["type"] != "reverse"]
    rev = [r for r in rs if r["type"] == "reverse"]

    atom = {
        "concept": name,
        "answer": c["answer"],
        "idx": CONCEPT_TO_IDX[name],
        "kind": c["kind"],

        "amp_c": mean_or_zero(rs, "amp_c", R_C),
        "amp_k": mean_or_zero(rs, "amp_k", R_K),

        "sensA_c": mean_or_zero(fwd, "sens_c", R_C),
        "sensA_k": mean_or_zero(fwd, "sens_k", R_K),

        "sensR_c": mean_or_zero(rev, "sens_c", R_C),
        "sensR_k": mean_or_zero(rev, "sens_k", R_K),

        "hash_raw": mean_or_zero(rs, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM),
        "hash_masked": mean_or_zero(rs, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM),
    }

    atoms.append(atom)

print(f"✅ DNA atoms built: {len(atoms)} concepts")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Train DNA-only routers: hash -> wave/sensitivity features
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def ridge_fit(X, Y, lam=1e-2, out_dim=None):
    if X.shape[0] == 0:
        od = out_dim if out_dim is not None else (Y.shape[1] if Y.ndim > 1 and Y.shape[0] > 0 else 0)
        return torch.zeros(X.shape[1] + 1, od, dtype=torch.float32)

    X = X.double()
    Y = Y.double()

    bias = torch.ones(X.shape[0], 1, dtype=X.dtype)
    Xa = torch.cat([X, bias], dim=1)

    A = Xa.T @ Xa
    A = A + lam * torch.eye(A.shape[0], dtype=X.dtype)

    B = Xa.T @ Y

    try:
        W = torch.linalg.solve(A, B)
    except Exception:
        W = torch.linalg.lstsq(A, B).solution

    return W.float()


def ridge_predict(X, W):
    if W.shape[1] == 0:
        return torch.zeros(X.shape[0], 0, dtype=torch.float32)

    X = X.double()
    bias = torch.ones(X.shape[0], 1, dtype=X.dtype)
    Xa = torch.cat([X, bias], dim=1)
    return (Xa @ W.double()).float()


def stack_tensor(rows, key, dim):
    if len(rows) == 0:
        return torch.zeros((0, dim), dtype=torch.float32)
    return torch.stack([r[key] for r in rows], dim=0).float()


country_fwd = [r for r in country_train if r["type"] != "reverse"]
country_rev = [r for r in country_train if r["type"] == "reverse"]

chemical_fwd = [r for r in chemical_train if r["type"] != "reverse"]
chemical_rev = [r for r in chemical_train if r["type"] == "reverse"]

# Raw/masked inputs
Xc_raw = stack_tensor(country_train, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xc_mask = stack_tensor(country_train, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)
Xk_raw = stack_tensor(chemical_train, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xk_mask = stack_tensor(chemical_train, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)

Xcf_raw = stack_tensor(country_fwd, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xcf_mask = stack_tensor(country_fwd, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)
Xkf_raw = stack_tensor(chemical_fwd, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xkf_mask = stack_tensor(chemical_fwd, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)

Xcr_raw = stack_tensor(country_rev, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xcr_mask = stack_tensor(country_rev, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)
Xkr_raw = stack_tensor(chemical_rev, "hash_raw", HASH_DIM)
Xkr_mask = stack_tensor(chemical_rev, "hash_masked", HASH_DIM)

# Targets
Yamp_c = stack_tensor(country_train, "amp_c", R_C)
Yamp_k = stack_tensor(chemical_train, "amp_k", R_K)

YsensA_c = stack_tensor(country_fwd, "sens_c", R_C)
YsensA_k = stack_tensor(chemical_fwd, "sens_k", R_K)

YsensR_c = stack_tensor(country_rev, "sens_c", R_C)
YsensR_k = stack_tensor(chemical_rev, "sens_k", R_K)

# Routers
W_amp_c_raw = ridge_fit(Xc_raw, Yamp_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)
W_amp_c_mask = ridge_fit(Xc_mask, Yamp_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)

W_amp_k_raw = ridge_fit(Xk_raw, Yamp_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)
W_amp_k_mask = ridge_fit(Xk_mask, Yamp_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)

W_sensA_c_raw = ridge_fit(Xcf_raw, YsensA_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)
W_sensA_c_mask = ridge_fit(Xcf_mask, YsensA_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)

W_sensA_k_raw = ridge_fit(Xkf_raw, YsensA_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)
W_sensA_k_mask = ridge_fit(Xkf_mask, YsensA_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)

W_sensR_c_raw = ridge_fit(Xcr_raw, YsensR_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)
W_sensR_c_mask = ridge_fit(Xcr_mask, YsensR_c, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_C)

W_sensR_k_raw = ridge_fit(Xkr_raw, YsensR_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)
W_sensR_k_mask = ridge_fit(Xkr_mask, YsensR_k, lam=1e-2, out_dim=R_K)

print("✅ DNA-only raw + masked routers trained")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Delete teacher model. Inference is DNA-only from here.
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n🧹 Deleting teacher model. Inference will use DNA arrays only...")

del model
del tok
gc.collect()

print(f"✅ Teacher deleted. Memory freed. ({time.time() - t0:.1f}s)")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. Lens definitions and scoring
# ------------------------------------------------------------
LENS_NAMES = [
    "raw_hash",
    "masked_hash",
    "wave_c_raw",
    "wave_c_mask",
    "wave_k_raw",
    "wave_k_mask",
    "sensA_c_raw",
    "sensA_c_mask",
    "sensA_k_raw",
    "sensA_k_mask",
    "sensR_c_raw",
    "sensR_c_mask",
    "sensR_k_raw",
    "sensR_k_mask",
]
LENS_TO_IDX = {n: i for i, n in enumerate(LENS_NAMES)}
N_LENS = len(LENS_NAMES)


def cos_1d(a, b):
    a = a.float()
    b = b.float()
    na = float(a.norm().item())
    nb = float(b.norm().item())
    if na < 1e-8 or nb < 1e-8:
        return 0.0
    return float((a * b).sum().item() / (na * nb))


def compute_row_lens(row):
    hraw = row["hash_raw"].float()
    hmask = row["hash_masked"].float()

    pred_amp_c_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_c_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_amp_c_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_c_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_amp_k_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_k_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_amp_k_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_amp_k_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_sensA_c_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_sensA_c_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_sensA_c_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_sensA_c_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_sensA_k_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_sensA_k_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_sensA_k_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_sensA_k_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_sensR_c_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_sensR_c_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_sensR_c_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_sensR_c_mask).squeeze(0)

    pred_sensR_k_raw = ridge_predict(hraw.unsqueeze(0), W_sensR_k_raw).squeeze(0)
    pred_sensR_k_mask = ridge_predict(hmask.unsqueeze(0), W_sensR_k_mask).squeeze(0)

    lens = torch.zeros(len(atoms), N_LENS, dtype=torch.float32)

    for ai, atom in enumerate(atoms):
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["raw_hash"]] = cos_1d(hraw, atom["hash_raw"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["masked_hash"]] = cos_1d(hmask, atom["hash_masked"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_amp_c_raw, atom["amp_c"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_amp_c_mask, atom["amp_c"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_amp_k_raw, atom["amp_k"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_amp_k_mask, atom["amp_k"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_c_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensA_c_raw, atom["sensA_c"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_c_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensA_c_mask, atom["sensA_c"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_k_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensA_k_raw, atom["sensA_k"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_k_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensA_k_mask, atom["sensA_k"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_c_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensR_c_raw, atom["sensR_c"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_c_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensR_c_mask, atom["sensR_c"])

        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_k_raw"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensR_k_raw, atom["sensR_k"])
        lens[ai, LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_k_mask"]] = cos_1d(pred_sensR_k_mask, atom["sensR_k"])

    row["lens_scores"] = lens
    row["feat_full"] = lens.reshape(-1)


for r in all_rows:
    compute_row_lens(r)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. Train learned fusion classifiers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def one_hot(indices, n_classes):
    Y = torch.zeros(len(indices), n_classes, dtype=torch.float32)
    Y[torch.arange(len(indices)), indices] = 1.0
    return Y


def subset_features(feat, lens_keep):
    ls = feat.view(len(atoms), N_LENS)[:, lens_keep]
    return ls.reshape(-1)


train_indices = [CONCEPT_TO_IDX[r["concept"]] for r in train_rows]
Yfusion = one_hot(train_indices, N_CONCEPTS)

FUSION_SUBSETS = {
    "fusion_all": list(range(N_LENS)),
    "fusion_no_hash": list(range(2, N_LENS)),
    "semantic_masked": [
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_k_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_k_mask"],
    ],
    "semantic_raw": [
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_raw"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_raw"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_c_raw"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_k_raw"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_c_raw"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_k_raw"],
    ],
    "forward_masked": [
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensA_k_mask"],
    ],
    "reverse_masked": [
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["wave_k_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_c_mask"],
        LENS_TO_IDX["sensR_k_mask"],
    ],
}

W_fusion = {}

for fname, lenses in FUSION_SUBSETS.items():
    Xsub = torch.stack([subset_features(r["feat_full"], lenses) for r in train_rows], dim=0)
    W_fusion[fname] = ridge_fit(Xsub, Yfusion, lam=1.0, out_dim=N_CONCEPTS)

print("✅ Learned fusion classifiers trained")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 13. Prediction helpers
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def predict_single_lens(row, lens_name):
    idx = LENS_TO_IDX[lens_name]
    scores = row["lens_scores"][:, idx]
    best = int(torch.argmax(scores).item())
    return atoms[best]["concept"], scores


def predict_fusion(row, fusion_name):
    lenses = FUSION_SUBSETS[fusion_name]
    x = subset_features(row["feat_full"], lenses).unsqueeze(0)
    logits = ridge_predict(x, W_fusion[fusion_name]).squeeze(0)
    best = int(torch.argmax(logits).item())
    return atoms[best]["concept"], logits


def display_target_for_prompt(row, pred_concept):
    if row["type"] == "reverse":
        return pred_concept
    return ANSWER_MAP.get(pred_concept, "?")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 1: Mode accuracy by prompt type
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 1: DNA-ONLY MODE ACCURACY BY PROMPT TYPE")
print("=" * 108)

TYPE_ORDER = ["direct", "alias", "clue", "reverse", "distractor"]

MODES = LENS_NAMES + list(FUSION_SUBSETS.keys())

print(
    f"{'Mode':<24}{'Overall%':<11}{'Direct%':<10}{'Alias%':<10}"
    f"{'Clue%':<10}{'Reverse%':<11}{'Distractor%'}"
)
print("-" * 108)

mode_results = {}

for mode in MODES:
    type_correct = {t: 0 for t in TYPE_ORDER}
    type_total = {t: 0 for t in TYPE_ORDER}
    overall_correct = 0

    for r in test_rows:
        if mode in LENS_TO_IDX:
            pred, _ = predict_single_lens(r, mode)
        else:
            pred, _ = predict_fusion(r, mode)

        typ = r["type"]
        type_total[typ] += 1

        if pred == r["concept"]:
            overall_correct += 1
            type_correct[typ] += 1

    overall_acc = overall_correct / max(1, len(test_rows))
    type_acc = {
        t: type_correct[t] / max(1, type_total[t])
        for t in TYPE_ORDER
    }

    mode_results[mode] = {
        "overall": overall_acc,
        "types": type_acc,
    }

    print(
        f"{mode:<24}{overall_acc * 100:<11.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['direct'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['alias'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['clue'] * 100:<10.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['reverse'] * 100:<11.1f}"
        f"{type_acc['distractor'] * 100:.1f}"
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 2: Country vs Chemical accuracy for key fusion modes
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 2: COUNTRY VS CHEMICAL ACCURACY")
print("=" * 108)

KEY_MODES = [
    "raw_hash",
    "masked_hash",
    "fusion_no_hash",
    "semantic_masked",
    "forward_masked",
    "reverse_masked",
]

print(f"{'Mode':<24}{'Country%':<12}{'Chemical%':<12}{'Overall%'}")
print("-" * 62)

kind_results = {}

for mode in KEY_MODES:
    country_correct = 0
    country_total = 0
    chem_correct = 0
    chem_total = 0

    for r in test_rows:
        if mode in LENS_TO_IDX:
            pred, _ = predict_single_lens(r, mode)
        else:
            pred, _ = predict_fusion(r, mode)

        kind = CONCEPT_KIND[r["concept"]]
        ok = (pred == r["concept"])

        if kind == "country":
            country_total += 1
            country_correct += int(ok)
        else:
            chem_total += 1
            chem_correct += int(ok)

    country_acc = country_correct / max(1, country_total)
    chem_acc = chem_correct / max(1, chem_total)
    overall_acc = mode_results[mode]["overall"]

    kind_results[mode] = (country_acc, chem_acc, overall_acc)

    print(
        f"{mode:<24}{country_acc * 100:<12.1f}"
        f"{chem_acc * 100:<12.1f}{overall_acc * 100:.1f}"
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 3: Detailed predictions — semantic_masked
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 3: DETAILED PREDICTIONS — semantic_masked")
print("   (No raw hash lens; masked semantic routers only)")
print("=" * 108)

print(f"{'Type':<12}{'Prompt':<58}{'True':<12}{'Pred':<12}{'Display':<16}{'OK'}")
print("-" * 128)

semantic_masked_correct = 0

for r in test_rows:
    pred, logits = predict_fusion(r, "semantic_masked")
    disp = display_target_for_prompt(r, pred)
    ok = (pred == r["concept"])
    if ok:
        semantic_masked_correct += 1

    short_p = r["prompt"] if len(r["prompt"]) <= 56 else r["prompt"][:53] + "..."
    mark = "🟢" if ok else "🔴"

    print(
        f"{r['type']:<12}{short_p:<58}{r['concept']:<12}{pred:<12}{disp:<16}{mark}"
    )

semantic_masked_detail_acc = semantic_masked_correct / max(1, len(test_rows))
print(f"\nsemantic_masked detailed accuracy: {semantic_masked_detail_acc * 100:.1f}%")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 4: Discriminative margin by prompt type — fusion_no_hash
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 4: DISCRIMINATIVE MARGIN — fusion_no_hash")
print("   Margin = top logit - second logit")
print("=" * 108)

margin_by_type = {t: [] for t in TYPE_ORDER}

for r in test_rows:
    _, logits = predict_fusion(r, "fusion_no_hash")
    top2 = torch.topk(logits, 2)
    margin = float((top2.values[0] - top2.values[1]).item())
    margin_by_type[r["type"]].append(margin)

print(f"{'Type':<14}{'AvgMargin':<12}{'MinMargin':<12}{'MaxMargin':<12}{'Verdict'}")
print("-" * 78)

for t in TYPE_ORDER:
    vals = margin_by_type[t]
    if not vals:
        continue
    avg = float(np.mean(vals))
    mn = float(np.min(vals))
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    verdict = "🟢" if avg >= 0.20 else ("🟡" if avg >= 0.08 else "🔴")
    print(f"{t:<14}{avg:<12.4f}{mn:<12.4f}{mx:<12.4f}{verdict}")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 5: Unknown concept rejection diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 5: UNKNOWN CONCEPT REJECTION DIAGNOSTIC")
print("=" * 108)

UNKNOWN_PROMPTS = [
    "The capital of Atlantis is",
    "The capital of Nowhereland is",
    "The chemical symbol for Unobtainium is",
    "The chemical symbol for Fictionium is",
    "The capital of Moonville is",
    "Which element has atomic number 999",
]

print(f"{'Prompt':<50}{'TopPred':<14}{'RawMargin':<12}{'FusionMargin':<15}{'Status'}")
print("-" * 112)

for p in UNKNOWN_PROMPTS:
    row = {
        "prompt": p,
        "concept": None,
        "type": "unknown",
        "split": "unknown",
        "target_text": "",
        "mask_terms": [],
    }
    row["hash_raw"] = char_hash_features(p)
    row["hash_masked"] = char_hash_features(mask_text(p, []))
    compute_row_lens(row)

    raw_scores = row["lens_scores"][:, LENS_TO_IDX["raw_hash"]]
    raw_top2 = torch.topk(raw_scores, 2)
    raw_margin = float((raw_top2.values[0] - raw_top2.values[1]).item())

    pred_fusion, logits = predict_fusion(row, "fusion_no_hash")
    probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
    top2p = torch.topk(probs, 2)
    fusion_margin = float((top2p.values[0] - top2p.values[1]).item())

    if fusion_margin < 0.05:
        status = "🟡 LOW CONFIDENCE"
    elif fusion_margin < 0.15:
        status = "🟠 WEAK CONFIDENCE"
    else:
        status = "🔴 OVERCONFIDENT"

    short_p = p if len(p) <= 48 else p[:45] + "..."
    print(
        f"{short_p:<50}{pred_fusion:<14}{raw_margin:<12.4f}"
        f"{fusion_margin:<15.4f}{status}"
    )

print("\nNote: Unknown concepts are not in DNA memory. Good DNA should show low margin.")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST 6: 1MB DNA size budget
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🧪 TEST 6: 1MB DNA SIZE BUDGET")
print("=" * 108)

basis_params = (R_C + R_K + 2) * M

# 12 routers:
# amp_c raw/mask, amp_k raw/mask,
# sensA_c raw/mask, sensA_k raw/mask,
# sensR_c raw/mask, sensR_k raw/mask
router_params = 2 * (3 * R_C + 3 * R_K) * (HASH_DIM + 1)

atom_params = N_CONCEPTS * (
    3 * R_C + 3 * R_K + 2 * HASH_DIM
)

answer_text_params = sum(len(c["answer"]) + len(c["name"]) for c in CONCEPTS)

fusion_features = N_CONCEPTS * N_LENS
fusion_params = (fusion_features + 1) * N_CONCEPTS

meta_params = 128

total_params = (
    basis_params +
    router_params +
    atom_params +
    answer_text_params +
    fusion_params +
    meta_params
)

fp16_bytes = total_params * 2
fp16_mb = fp16_bytes / 1_000_000.0

print(f"Country wave rank              : {R_C}")
print(f"Chemical wave rank             : {R_K}")
print(f"Response wave dimension        : {M}")
print(f"Number of DNA atoms            : {N_CONCEPTS}")
print(f"Hash encoder dim               : {HASH_DIM}")
print()
print(f"Wave basis params              : {basis_params:,}")
print(f"Router params                  : {router_params:,}")
print(f"Atom params                    : {atom_params:,}")
print(f"Answer text params (rough)     : {answer_text_params:,}")
print(f"Fusion classifier params       : {fusion_params:,}")
print(f"Meta params                    : {meta_params:,}")
print(f"Total params                   : {total_params:,}")
print(f"fp16 estimated size            : {fp16_mb:.3f} MB")

if fp16_mb <= 1.0:
    print("✅ This PD-5 anti-shortcut DNA prototype fits under 1MB.")
else:
    print("🔴 Current prototype exceeds 1MB; reduce rank/hash dim/features.")


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# FINAL VERDICT
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 108)
print("🏆 PD-5 FINAL VERDICT")
print("=" * 108)

raw_hash_res = mode_results["raw_hash"]

if fp16_mb <= 1.0:
    print("✅ SIZE: narrow-domain anti-shortcut DNA cartridge can fit under 1MB.")
else:

# ============================================================
# 🧬 PD-6: HYBRID CLOSED-BOOK DNA CARTRIDGE — ONE CELL CPU
# ============================================================
# Goal:
# Teacher model se knowledge extract karo, teacher delete karo,
# phir DNA-only hybrid system se stored concepts retrieve karo:
#   - direct prompts
#   - alias prompts
#   - clue prompts
#   - reverse prompts
#   - distractor prompts
#   - unknown rejection
#
# Hybrid DNA components:
# 1. Per-domain activation wave basis
# 2. Masked semantic routers
# 3. Symbolic n-gram index memory
# 4. Scalable linear fusion
# 5. Margin-based unknown rejection
# 6. 1MB size budget + scale estimate
#
# Important:
# This is a narrow-domain stored-knowledge cartridge prototype.
# It does NOT prove full general LLM replacement.
# ============================================================

import os
import gc
import math
import time
import hashlib
from collections import defaultdict

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

try:
    torch.set_num_threads(max(1, (os.cpu_count() or 1) - 1))
except Exception:
    pass

torch.manual_seed(123)
np.random.seed(123)

t0 = time.time()

print("=" * 112)
print("🧬 PD-6: HYBRID CLOSED-BOOK DNA CARTRIDGE — ONE CELL CPU PROTOTYPE")
print("=" * 112)

MODEL = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct"
HASH_DIM = 256

# Use selected layers to keep wave basis smaller.
# Full 24-layer wave basis was too heavy for scaling.
SELECTED_LAYER_CANDIDATES = [0, 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 23]

R_COUNTRY_DESIRED = 6
R_CHEMICAL_DESIRED = 10
