"""
DECISIVE EXPERIMENT: Real teacher → MDL rule induction → HELIX artifact → DNA-only student
Tests: Is D_derive > 3 reachable in a real domain with automatic compiler?
COLAB COMPATIBLE VERSION
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import re
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Optional
import numpy as np

# Colab/Jupyter compatible path setup
current_dir = os.getcwd()
if current_dir not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, current_dir)

from helix import KnowledgeDNA, RelSpec
from evaluate import evaluate, tuple_bit_complexity, llm_artifact_bytes

# ============================================================================
# CONFIG
# ============================================================================
N_CONCEPTS = 100  # Start with 100 concepts (scale to 1000 later)
N_RELATIONS = 5   # capital_of, symbol_of, continent_of, country_of, category_of
N_PARAPHRASES = 5 # direct, alias, clue, reverse, chain
USE_REAL_TEACHER = False  # False = mock teacher (fast), True = Qwen 0.5B (slow)

# ============================================================================
# STEP 1: LOAD TEACHER
# ============================================================================
def load_teacher():
    """Load Qwen 0.5B or mock teacher."""
    if not USE_REAL_TEACHER:
        print("⚡ Using MOCK teacher (fast mode)")
        return MockTeacher()
    
    print("🔄 Loading Qwen 0.5B-Instruct (this may take 1-2 minutes on CPU)...")
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
    
    model_name = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct"
    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_name, torch_dtype="auto", device_map="auto")
    model.eval()
    
    print(f"✅ Teacher loaded: {model_name}")
    return RealTeacher(model, tokenizer)


class MockTeacher:
    """Mock teacher for fast testing (no GPU needed)."""
    def __init__(self):
        # Hardcoded knowledge for 100 countries + 118 elements
        self.knowledge = self._build_knowledge()
    
    def _build_knowledge(self):
        """Build knowledge base from world.py data."""
        import world
        kb = {}
        
        # Countries
        for line in world.COUNTRIES.strip().splitlines():
            parts = line.split("|")
            if len(parts) != 3:
                continue
            country, capital, continent = [p.strip() for p in parts]
            kb[f"{country}|capital_of"] = capital
            kb[f"{country}|continent_of"] = continent
            kb[f"{capital}|country_of"] = country
        
        # Elements
        for i, line in enumerate(world.ELEMENTS.strip().split("|"), start=1):
            parts = line.split(None, 2)
            if len(parts) != 3:
                continue
            symbol, name, category = parts
            kb[f"{name}|symbol_of"] = symbol
            kb[f"{name}|number_of"] = str(i)
            kb[f"{name}|category_of"] = category
        
        return kb
    
    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_new_tokens: int = 8) -> str:
        """Generate answer from knowledge base."""
        prompt_lower = prompt.lower()
        
        # Try to match patterns
        for key, answer in self.knowledge.items():
            entity, relation = key.split("|")
            if entity.lower() in prompt_lower:
                if relation == "capital_of" and "capital" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
                elif relation == "continent_of" and "continent" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
                elif relation == "country_of" and "country" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
                elif relation == "symbol_of" and "symbol" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
                elif relation == "number_of" and "number" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
                elif relation == "category_of" and "category" in prompt_lower:
                    return answer
        
        return "I don't know."


class RealTeacher:
    """Real Qwen 0.5B teacher."""
    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer):
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
    
    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_new_tokens: int = 8) -> str:
        """Generate answer using Qwen 0.5B."""
        import torch
        inputs = self.tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(self.model.device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            outputs = self.model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
                do_sample=False,
                pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id
            )
        answer = self.tokenizer.decode(outputs[0][inputs.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)
        return answer.strip()


# ============================================================================
# STEP 2: GENERATE PROBE GRID
# ============================================================================
def generate_probe_grid(concepts: List[str]) -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    """
    Generate probe grid: (prompt, entity, relation)
    100 concepts × 5 relations × 5 paraphrases = 2500 probes
    """
    probes = []
    
    # Import world data for aliases and clues
    import world
    
    for concept in concepts:
        # Determine if country or element
        is_country = concept in [line.split("|")[0].strip() for line in world.COUNTRIES.strip().splitlines()]
        
        if is_country:
            # Country relations
            relations = ["capital_of", "continent_of"]
        else:
            # Element relations
            relations = ["symbol_of", "number_of", "category_of"]
        
        for relation in relations:
            # Generate 5 paraphrases
            paraphrases = generate_paraphrases(concept, relation, world.ALIASES, world.CLUES)
            for prompt in paraphrases:
                probes.append((prompt, concept, relation))
    
    return probes


def generate_paraphrases(entity: str, relation: str, aliases: Dict[str, str], clues: Dict[str, str]) -> List[str]:
    """Generate 5 paraphrases for a given entity-relation pair."""
    paraphrases = []
    
    # Find alias for this entity
    alias = None
    for a, canon in aliases.items():
        if canon == entity:
            alias = a
            break
    
    # Find clue for this entity
    clue = None
    for c, canon in clues.items():
        if canon == entity:
            clue = c
            break
    
    if relation == "capital_of":
        paraphrases = [
            f"What is the capital of {entity}?",  # direct
            f"What is the capital of {alias}?" if alias else f"What is the capital of {entity}?",  # alias
            f"What is the capital of the {clue} country?" if clue else f"What is the capital of {entity}?",  # clue
            f"Which country has {entity}'s capital as its capital?",  # reverse (broken, will fail)
            f"What is the capital of the country of {entity}'s capital?",  # chain (2-hop)
        ]
    elif relation == "continent_of":
        paraphrases = [
            f"What continent is {entity} in?",
            f"What continent is {alias} in?" if alias else f"What continent is {entity} in?",
            f"What continent is the {clue} country in?" if clue else f"What continent is {entity} in?",
            f"Which country is in the same continent as {entity}?",  # reverse (broken)
            f"What continent is the country of {entity} in?",  # chain
        ]
    elif relation == "symbol_of":
        paraphrases = [
            f"What is the chemical symbol of {entity}?",
            f"What is the symbol of {alias}?" if alias else f"What is the symbol of {entity}?",
            f"What is the symbol of the {clue} element?" if clue else f"What is the symbol of {entity}?",
            f"Which element has the symbol {entity}?",  # reverse
            f"What is the symbol of the element {entity}?",  # chain
        ]
    elif relation == "number_of":
        paraphrases = [
            f"What is the atomic number of {entity}?",
            f"What is the number of {alias}?" if alias else f"What is the number of {entity}?",
            f"What is the atomic number of the {clue} element?" if clue else f"What is the atomic number of {entity}?",
            f"Which element has atomic number {entity}?",  # reverse
            f"What is the atomic number of {entity}?",  # chain
        ]
    elif relation == "category_of":
        paraphrases = [
            f"What category of element is {entity}?",
            f"What category is {alias}?" if alias else f"What category is {entity}?",
            f"What category is the {clue} element?" if clue else f"What category is {entity}?",
            f"Which elements are {entity}?",  # reverse
            f"What category does {entity} belong to?",  # chain
        ]
    
    return paraphrases[:N_PARAPHRASES]


# ============================================================================
# STEP 3: EXTRACT ATOMS FROM TEACHER
# ============================================================================
def extract_atoms(teacher, probes: List[Tuple[str, str, str]]) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str], str]:
    """
    Extract atoms from teacher: (entity, relation) → answer
    Group probes by (entity, relation), take modal answer.
    """
    print(f"\n🔬 Extracting atoms from {len(probes)} probes...")
    
    buckets = defaultdict(list)
    
    for i, (prompt, entity, relation) in enumerate(probes):
        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:
            print(f"  Progress: {i + 1}/{len(probes)}")
        
        answer = teacher.generate(prompt, max_new_tokens=8)
        buckets[(entity, relation)].append(answer)
    
    # Take modal answer for each (entity, relation)
    atoms = {}
    for (entity, relation), answers in buckets.items():
        if len(answers) < 2:
            continue  # Need at least 2 answers for modal
        
        # Find most common answer
        counter = Counter(answers)
        modal_answer, count = counter.most_common(1)[0]
        
        # Only keep if at least 50% agreement
        if count >= len(answers) * 0.5:
            atoms[(entity, relation)] = modal_answer
    
    print(f"✅ Extracted {len(atoms)} atoms (from {len(buckets)} buckets)")
    return atoms


# ============================================================================
# STEP 4: MDL RULE INDUCTION (GREEDY)
# ============================================================================
def induce_rules(atoms: Dict[Tuple[str, str], str]) -> Dict:
    """
    Greedy MDL rule induction:
    1. Find inverse relations
    2. Find 2-hop chains
    3. Find alias closure
    """
    print(f"\n🧠 Inducing rules from {len(atoms)} atoms...")
    
    rules = {
        "inverse": {},
        "chain": {},
        "alias": {}
    }
    
    # Step 1: Find inverse relations
    # If (A, r1) → B and (B, r2) → A, then r2 = inverse(r1)
    print("  Step 1: Finding inverse relations...")
    for (e1, r1), o1 in atoms.items():
        for (e2, r2), o2 in atoms.items():
            if e1 == o2 and e2 == o1 and r1 != r2:
                # Found inverse
                if r1 not in rules["inverse"]:
                    rules["inverse"][r1] = r2
                    print(f"    Found: {r2} is inverse of {r1}")
    
    # Step 2: Find 2-hop chains
    # If (A, r1) → B and (B, r2) → C, then (A, r1∘r2) → C
    print("  Step 2: Finding 2-hop chains...")
    chain_count = 0
    for (e1, r1), o1 in atoms.items():
        for (e2, r2), o2 in atoms.items():
            if o1 == e2:
                # Found chain: e1 -r1-> o1=e2 -r2-> o2
                chain_rel = f"{r1}_then_{r2}"
                rules["chain"][(e1, chain_rel)] = o2
                chain_count += 1
    
    print(f"    Found {chain_count} 2-hop chains")
    
    # Step 3: Find alias closure
    # If alias(A) = B and (B, r) → C, then (A, r) → C
    print("  Step 3: Finding alias closure...")
    import world
    alias_count = 0
    for alias, canon in world.ALIASES.items():
        for (e, r), o in atoms.items():
            if e == canon:
                rules["alias"][(alias, r)] = o
                alias_count += 1
    
    print(f"    Found {alias_count} alias closures")
    
    print(f"✅ Induced rules: {len(rules['inverse'])} inverses, {len(rules['chain'])} chains, {len(rules['alias'])} aliases")
    return rules


# ============================================================================
# STEP 5: PACK ARTIFACT
# ============================================================================
def pack_artifact(atoms: Dict[Tuple[str, str], str], rules: Dict) -> KnowledgeDNA:
    """Pack atoms + rules into HELIX artifact."""
    print(f"\n📦 Packing artifact...")
    
    # Convert atoms to facts format
    facts = [(e, r, o) for (e, r), o in atoms.items()]
    
    # Import world data
    import world
    
    # Create DNA
    dna = KnowledgeDNA()
    
    # Add relation specs
    for rel_name in set(r for (_, r) in atoms.keys()):
        dna.rules.add_rel(RelSpec(rel_name))
    
    # Add inverse relations
    for r1, r2 in rules["inverse"].items():
        if r1 in dna.rules.rels:
            dna.rules.inverse_of[r2] = r1
    
    # Add aliases
    for alias, canon in world.ALIASES.items():
        dna.rules.aliases[alias.lower()] = canon
    
    # Add clues
    for clue, canon in world.CLUES.items():
        dna.rules.clues[clue.lower()] = canon
    
    # Compile
    dna.compile_from_teacher(facts, aliases=world.ALIASES, clues=world.CLUES, rels=world.REL_SPECS)
    
    # Save artifact
    artifact_path = "artifacts/decisive_experiment.dna"
    os.makedirs("artifacts", exist_ok=True)
    dna.save(artifact_path)
    
    artifact_size = os.path.getsize(artifact_path)
    print(f"✅ Artifact saved: {artifact_path} ({artifact_size:,} bytes)")
    
    return dna


# ============================================================================
# STEP 6: RUN DNA-ONLY STUDENT
# ============================================================================
def run_dna_student(dna: KnowledgeDNA, probes: List[Tuple[str, str, str]]) -> Dict:
    """Run DNA-only student on probes and measure metrics."""
    print(f"\n🧪 Running DNA-only student on {len(probes)} probes...")
    
    correct = 0
    total = 0
    unknown_correct = 0
    unknown_total = 0
    
    for i, (prompt, entity, relation) in enumerate(probes):
        if (i + 1) % 100 == 0:
            print(f"  Progress: {i + 1}/{len(probes)}")
        
        # Encode query
        parsed = dna.encode_query(prompt)
        
        # Retrieve
        ret = dna.retrieve(parsed)
        
        # Check if unknown
        if ret.answer is None:
            unknown_total += 1
            # For now, we don't have ground truth for unknowns
            # In real experiment, we'd add unknown probes
            continue
        
        # Check answer
        total += 1
        if ret.answer.lower().strip() == entity.lower().strip():
            correct += 1
    
    accuracy = correct / max(1, total)
    unknown_accuracy = unknown_correct / max(1, unknown_total)
    
    print(f"✅ DNA-only student: {correct}/{total} correct ({accuracy:.1%})")
    
    return {
        "accuracy": accuracy,
        "correct": correct,
        "total": total,
        "unknown_accuracy": unknown_accuracy,
        "unknown_correct": unknown_correct,
        "unknown_total": unknown_total
    }


# ============================================================================
# STEP 7: COMPUTE METRICS
# ============================================================================
def compute_metrics(dna: KnowledgeDNA, atoms: Dict, rules: Dict, student_metrics: Dict) -> Dict:
    """Compute final metrics: D_derive, bits/answer, etc."""
    print(f"\n📊 Computing metrics...")
    
    # Count stored atoms
    n_atoms = len(atoms)
    
    # Count answerable tuples (atoms + derived)
    n_tuples = n_atoms
    n_tuples += len(rules["inverse"])  # Inverse relations
    n_tuples += len(rules["chain"])     # 2-hop chains
    n_tuples += len(rules["alias"])     # Alias closures
    
    # Compute D_derive
    D_derive = n_tuples / max(1, n_atoms)
    
    # Compute artifact size
    artifact_path = "artifacts/decisive_experiment.dna"
    artifact_size = os.path.getsize(artifact_path)
    
    # Compute bits per answer
    bits_per_answer = (artifact_size * 8) / max(1, n_tuples)
    
    # Compute bits per stored bit
    knowledge_bits = tuple_bit_complexity([(e, r, o) for (e, r), o in atoms.items()], len(dna.rules.rels)) * n_tuples
    stored_bits = artifact_size * 8
    bits_per_stored_bit = knowledge_bits / max(1, stored_bits)
    
    # Compare to int8 teacher
    int8_bits_per_param = 0.25
    int8_teacher_size = llm_artifact_bytes(knowledge_bits, 8)
    density_vs_int8 = bits_per_stored_bit / int8_bits_per_param
    
    metrics = {
        "n_atoms": n_atoms,
        "n_tuples": n_tuples,
        "D_derive": D_derive,
        "artifact_size": artifact_size,
        "bits_per_answer": bits_per_answer,
        "bits_per_stored_bit": bits_per_stored_bit,
        "density_vs_int8": density_vs_int8,
        "student_accuracy": student_metrics["accuracy"],
        "student_correct": student_metrics["correct"],
        "student_total": student_metrics["total"],
    }
    
    print(f"✅ Metrics computed:")
    print(f"  Stored atoms: {n_atoms}")
    print(f"  Answerable tuples: {n_tuples}")
    print(f"  D_derive: {D_derive:.2f}")
    print(f"  Artifact size: {artifact_size:,} bytes")
    print(f"  Bits per answer: {bits_per_answer:.2f}")
    print(f"  Bits per stored bit: {bits_per_stored_bit:.3f}")
    print(f"  Density vs int8 teacher: {density_vs_int8:.2f}x")
    print(f"  Student accuracy: {student_metrics['accuracy']:.1%}")
    
    return metrics


# ============================================================================
# STEP 8: DECISION GATE
# ============================================================================
def decision_gate(metrics: Dict) -> bool:
    """Check if experiment passes decision gate."""
    print(f"\n🎯 Decision gate check...")
    
    # Gate 1: D_derive > 3
    if metrics["D_derive"] < 3.0:
        print(f"  ❌ FAIL: D_derive = {metrics['D_derive']:.2f} < 3.0")
        return False
    print(f"  ✅ PASS: D_derive = {metrics['D_derive']:.2f} > 3.0")
    
    # Gate 2: Student accuracy > 95%
    if metrics["student_accuracy"] < 0.95:
        print(f"  ❌ FAIL: Student accuracy = {metrics['student_accuracy']:.1%} < 95%")
        return False
    print(f"  ✅ PASS: Student accuracy = {metrics['student_accuracy']:.1%} > 95%")
    
    # Gate 3: Density vs int8 > 1.0x
    if metrics["density_vs_int8"] < 1.0:
        print(f"  ❌ FAIL: Density vs int8 = {metrics['density_vs_int8']:.2f}x < 1.0x")
        return False
    print(f"  ✅ PASS: Density vs int8 = {metrics['density_vs_int8']:.2f}x > 1.0x")
    
    print(f"\n🎉 ALL GATES PASSED! Experiment successful.")
    return True


# ============================================================================
# MAIN
# ============================================================================
def main():
    print("=" * 80)
    print("DECISIVE EXPERIMENT: Real teacher → MDL rule induction → HELIX artifact")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    start_time = time.time()
    
    # Step 1: Load teacher
    teacher = load_teacher()
    
    # Step 2: Generate probe grid
    import world
    concepts = [line.split("|")[0].strip() for line in world.COUNTRIES.strip().splitlines()][:N_CONCEPTS]
    concepts += [line.split(None, 2)[1] for line in world.ELEMENTS.strip().split("|")][:N_CONCEPTS]
    concepts = concepts[:N_CONCEPTS]  # Limit to N_CONCEPTS
    
    probes = generate_probe_grid(concepts)
    print(f"\n📋 Generated {len(probes)} probes for {len(concepts)} concepts")
    
    # Step 3: Extract atoms
    atoms = extract_atoms(teacher, probes)
    
    # Step 4: Induce rules
    rules = induce_rules(atoms)
    
    # Step 5: Pack artifact
    dna = pack_artifact(atoms, rules)
    
    # Step 6: Run DNA-only student
    student_metrics = run_dna_student(dna, probes)
    
    # Step 7: Compute metrics
    metrics = compute_metrics(dna, atoms, rules, student_metrics)
    
    # Step 8: Decision gate
    passed = decision_gate(metrics)
    
    # Save results
    results = {
        "metrics": metrics,
        "rules": {
            "n_inverse": len(rules["inverse"]),
            "n_chain": len(rules["chain"]),
            "n_alias": len(rules["alias"])
        },
        "passed": passed,
        "elapsed_time": time.time() - start_time
    }
    
    os.makedirs("artifacts", exist_ok=True)
    with open("artifacts/decisive_experiment_results.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)
    
    print(f"\n💾 Results saved to artifacts/decisive_experiment_results.json")
    print(f"⏱️ Total time: {results['elapsed_time']:.1f}s")
    
    if passed:
        print("\n🎉 SUCCESS: D_derive > 3 is reachable with automatic compiler!")
        print("   Next: Scale to 1000 concepts, add interference injection, write paper.")
    else:
        print("\n❌ FAILURE: D_derive > 3 not reachable with current approach.")
        print("   Next: Debug rule induction, try different domains, or accept limitation.")
    
    return passed


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import torch  # For RealTeacher
    main()# ============================================================
# PD-6/HELIX MINI — ONE CELL, NO FILE UPLOAD REQUIRED
# Run directly in Colab/Jupyter
# ============================================================

import re
import json
import math
import time
import hashlib
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional, List, Tuple, Dict, Iterable

print("=" * 100)
print("🧬 PD-6/HELIX MINI — ONE CELL KNOWLEDGE DNA PROTOTYPE")
print("=" * 100)

t0 = time.time()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Utils
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def norm(s: str) -> str:
    return " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", str(s).lower()).split())

def entity_index(name: str) -> Optional[int]:
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)\s*$", str(name))
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

def h64(*parts) -> int:
    m = hashlib.blake2b(digest_size=8)
    for p in parts:
        if isinstance(p, str):
            p = p.encode("utf-8")
        elif isinstance(p, int):
            p = str(p).encode("utf-8")
        m.update(p)
        m.update(b"\x1f")
    return int.from_bytes(m.digest(), "little")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dataclasses
# ------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class RelSpec:
    name: str
    patterns: List[Tuple[str, int, Optional[int]]] = field(default_factory=list)
    inverse_of: Optional[str] = None
    chain: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]] = None

@dataclass
class Parsed:
    subject: Optional[str]
    relation: Optional[str]
    confidence: float = 0.0
    raw: str = ""

@dataclass
class Retrieval:
    answer: Optional[str]
    subject: Optional[str]
    relation: Optional[str]
    confidence: float
    kind: str
    trace: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    tuples: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Vocab
# ------------------------------------------------------------
class Vocab:
    def __init__(self):
        self.names: List[str] = []
        self.ids: Dict[str, int] = {}

    def id(self, name: str) -> int:
        name = str(name)
        i = self.ids.get(name)
        if i is None:
            i = len(self.names)
            self.ids[name] = i
            self.names.append(name)
        return i

    def get(self, i: int) -> str:
        return self.names[i]

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.names)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Wave Lattice: content-addressed slot arena
# ------------------------------------------------------------
class WaveLattice:
    def __init__(self, n_slots: int = 4096, levels: int = 8, max_facts_per_cell: int = 32):
        self.n_slots = n_slots
        self.levels = levels
        self.max_facts_per_cell = max_facts_per_cell
        self.slots: Dict[int, dict] = {}

    def _slot_id(self, ent_id: int, level: int) -> int:
        return h64("slot", ent_id, level) % self.n_slots

    def write(self, ent_id: int, rel_id: int, obj_ref: int) -> None:
        for lvl in range(self.levels):
            sid = self._slot_id(ent_id, lvl)
            cell = self.slots.get(sid)

            if cell is None:
                self.slots[sid] = {
                    "ent": ent_id,
                    "level": lvl,
                    "facts": [(rel_id, obj_ref)]
                }
                return

            if cell["ent"] == ent_id and cell["level"] == lvl:
                if (rel_id, obj_ref) in cell["facts"]:
                    return
                if len(cell["facts"]) < self.max_facts_per_cell:
                    cell["facts"].append((rel_id, obj_ref))
                    return
                # full -> spiral to next level
                continue

            # foreign collision -> spiral next level
        raise RuntimeError("Spiral level overflow")

    def read(self, ent_id: int, rel_id: int) -> Optional[int]:
        for lvl in range(self.levels):
            sid = self._slot_id(ent_id, lvl)
            cell = self.slots.get(sid)
            if cell is None:
                continue
            if cell["ent"] != ent_id or cell["level"] != lvl:
                continue
            for r, o in cell["facts"]:
                if r == rel_id:
                    return o
        return None

    def iter_facts(self) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, int, int]]:
        for cell in self.slots.values():
            ent = cell["ent"]
            for r, o in cell["facts"]:
                yield ent, r, o

    def n_facts(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(c["facts"]) for c in self.slots.values())

    def size_estimate(self) -> int:
        # rough: header + 3 bytes per fact + cell overhead
        return sum(16 + 3 * len(c["facts"]) for c in self.slots.values())

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Rule Engine
# ------------------------------------------------------------
class RuleEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rels: Dict[str, RelSpec] = {}
        self.aliases: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.clues: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.gen_rules: Dict[str, List[dict]] = {}
        self.gen_entities: List[dict] = []

    def add_rel(self, spec: RelSpec):
        self.rels[spec.name] = spec

    def canon(self, name: str) -> str:
        return self.aliases.get(str(name).lower(), name)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Knowledge DNA
# ------------------------------------------------------------
class KnowledgeDNA:
    def __init__(self, n_slots: int = 4096):
        self.entities = Vocab()
        self.rules = RuleEngine()
        self.lattice = WaveLattice(n_slots=n_slots)
        self.rel_ids: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self.rel_names: Dict[int, str] = {}
        self.reverse_index: Dict[Tuple[int, int], int] = {}

    def compile_from_teacher(
        self,
        facts: Iterable[Tuple[str, str, str]],
        aliases: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        clues: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        rels: Optional[List[RelSpec]] = None,
        gen_rules: Optional[Dict[str, List[dict]]] = None,
        gen_entities: Optional[List[dict]] = None,
        n_slots: int = 4096,
    ):
        self.lattice = WaveLattice(n_slots=n_slots)
        self.entities = Vocab()
        self.rules = RuleEngine()
        self.rel_ids = {}
        self.rel_names = {}
        self.reverse_index = {}

        if rels:
            for r in rels:
                self.rules.add_rel(r)

        if aliases:
            for k, v in aliases.items():
                self.rules.aliases[str(k).lower()] = v

        if clues:
            for k, v in clues.items():
                self.rules.clues[str(k).lower()] = v

        if gen_rules:
            self.rules.gen_rules = gen_rules

        if gen_entities:
            self.rules.gen_entities = gen_entities

        prepared = []
        for s, r, o in facts:
            s = self.rules.canon(s)
            o = self.rules.canon(o)
            if r not in self.rel_ids:
                self.rel_ids[r] = len(self.rel_ids)
                self.rel_names[self.rel_ids[r]] = r
            prepared.append((s, self.rel_ids[r], o))

        for s, rid, o in prepared:
            ent = self.entities.id(s)
            obj = self.entities.id(o)
            self.lattice.write(ent, rid, obj)

        for ent, rid, obj in self.lattice.iter_facts():
            self.reverse_index.setdefault((rid, obj), ent)

        return self

    # -------- entity linking --------
    def _entity_vocab(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        v = {n.lower(): n for n in self.entities.names}
        for a, c in self.rules.aliases.items():
            v.setdefault(a.lower(), c)
        for c, n in self.rules.clues.items():
            v.setdefault(c.lower(), n)
        return v

    def _link_entities(self, text: str) -> List[Tuple[int, int, str]]:
        low = text.lower()
        vocab = self._entity_vocab()
        hits = []

        for surface, canon in vocab.items():
            if not surface:
                continue
            pat = r"(?<![a-z0-9])" + re.escape(surface) + r"(?![a-z0-9])"
            for m in re.finditer(pat, low):
                hits.append((m.start(), m.end(), canon))

        # generative entities like P0000
        for g in self.rules.gen_entities:
            for m in re.finditer(g["rx"], low):
                try:
                    idx = int(re.sub(r"\D", "", m.group(0)))
                except Exception:
                    continue
                canon = g["fmt"].format(idx)
                hits.append((m.start(), m.end(), canon))

        hits.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], -(t[1] - t[0])))
        chosen = []
        last_end = -1
        for i, j, c in hits:
            if i >= last_end:
                chosen.append((i, j, c))
                last_end = j
        return chosen

    @staticmethod
    def _pick_subject(mentions, m, group: int, text: str) -> Optional[str]:
        if not mentions:
            return None
        try:
            span = m.group(group)
        except Exception:
            span = None

        if span:
            inside = [c for i, j, c in mentions if m.start() <= i and j <= m.end()]
            if inside:
                return inside[0]

        mid = (m.start() + m.end()) / 2.0
        return min(mentions, key=lambda t: abs((t[0] + t[1]) / 2.0 - mid))[2]

    def encode_query(self, text: str) -> Parsed:
        low = text.lower()
        mentions = self._link_entities(text)
        best = None
        best_score = (-1, -1, -1)

        for name, spec in self.rules.rels.items():
            for pat, sgrp, _ in spec.patterns:
                m = re.search(pat, low)
                if not m:
                    continue

                is_chain = 1 if spec.chain else 0
                is_inv = 1 if spec.inverse_of else 0
                score = (is_chain, is_inv, len(m.group(0)))

                if score <= best_score:
                    continue

                subj = self._pick_subject(mentions, m, sgrp, text)
                if subj is None:
                    continue

                best = Parsed(subject=subj, relation=name, confidence=0.95, raw=text)
                best_score = score

        return best if best is not None else Parsed(None, None, 0.0, text)

    # -------- low-level reads --------
    def _gen_read(self, ent_name: str, rel_name: str):
        idx = entity_index(ent_name)
        if idx is None:
            return None

        for spec in self.rules.gen_rules.get(rel_name, []):
            table = spec.get("table", [])
            if not table:
                continue
            kind = spec.get("kind", "index_range")
            if kind == "index_range":
                q = int(spec.get("quot", 1))
                val = table[(idx // max(1, q)) % len(table)]
            else:
                val = table[idx % len(table)]
            return val, 0.90, [(ent_name, rel_name, val)]
        return None

    def _read(self, ent_name: str, rel_name: str):
        rid = self.rel_ids.get(rel_name)
        eid = self.entities.ids.get(ent_name)

        if eid is not None and rid is not None:
            obj = self.lattice.read(eid, rid)
            if obj is not None:
                ans = self.entities.get(obj)
                return ans, 1.00, [(ent_name, rel_name, ans)]

        g = self._gen_read(ent_name, rel_name)
        if g is not None:
            return g

        return None

    def _read_or_derive(self, ent_name: str, rel_name: str):
        got = self._read(ent_name, rel_name)
        if got is not None:
            return got

        spec = self.rules.rels.get(rel_name)
        if spec and spec.inverse_of:
            base = spec.inverse_of
            rid = self.rel_ids.get(base)
            eid = self.entities.ids.get(ent_name)
            if rid is not None and eid is not None:
                sub = self.reverse_index.get((rid, eid))
                if sub is not None:
                    ans = self.entities.get(sub)
                    # stored underlying claim + derived surface claim
                    return ans, 0.80, [(ans, base, ent_name), (ent_name, rel_name, ans)]
        return None

    # -------- retrieval --------
    def retrieve(self, parsed: Parsed) -> Retrieval:
        if not parsed.subject or not parsed.relation:
            return Retrieval(None, parsed.subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", ["parse_fail"], [])

        subject = self.rules.canon(parsed.subject)
        spec = self.rules.rels.get(parsed.relation)

        if spec and spec.chain:
            cur = subject
            tuples = []
            trace = [f"chain:{parsed.relation}"]
            for step in spec.chain:
                res = self._read_or_derive(cur, step)
                if res is None:
                    return Retrieval(None, subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", trace + [f"miss:{step}"], [])
                ans, score, tups = res
                trace.append(f"{cur} -[{step}]-> {ans}")
                tuples.extend(tups)
                cur = ans
            tuples.append((subject, parsed.relation, cur))
            return Retrieval(cur, subject, parsed.relation, 0.90, "derived", trace, tuples)

        res = self._read_or_derive(subject, parsed.relation)
        if res is not None:
            ans, score, tuples = res
            kind = "fact" if score >= 0.99 else "derived"
            return Retrieval(ans, subject, parsed.relation, score, kind, [f"{kind}:{subject}"], tuples)

        return Retrieval(None, subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", ["miss"], [])

    # -------- decode --------
    def decode(self, ret: Retrieval) -> str:
        if ret.answer is None:
            return "I don't know."
        templates = [
            "{a}",
            "The answer is {a}.",
            "It is {a}.",
            "According to memory: {a}.",
        ]
        h = int(hashlib.md5(str(ret.answer).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(), 16)
        return templates[h % len(templates)].format(a=ret.answer)

    # -------- size / stats --------
    def n_facts(self) -> int:
        return self.lattice.n_facts()

    def size_bytes(self) -> int:
        grammar_obj = {
            k: {
                "patterns": v.patterns,
                "inverse_of": v.inverse_of,
                "chain": list(v.chain) if v.chain else None,
            }
            for k, v in self.rules.rels.items()
        }
        lexicon_obj = {
            "aliases": self.rules.aliases,
            "clues": self.rules.clues,
            "gen_rules": self.rules.gen_rules,
            "gen_entities": self.rules.gen_entities,
        }
        namespace_obj = {"entities": self.entities.names}

        grammar = len(json.dumps(grammar_obj, sort_keys=True))
        lexicon = len(json.dumps(lexicon_obj, sort_keys=True))
        namespace = len(json.dumps(namespace_obj, sort_keys=True))
        lattice = self.lattice.size_estimate()
        return 64 + grammar + lexicon + namespace + lattice

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Relation specs
# ------------------------------------------------------------
REL_SPECS = [
    RelSpec(
        "capital_of",
        [
            (r"what is the capital of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 .'-]+)'s capital is", 1, None),
            (r"capital of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "continent_of",
        [
            (r"what continent is ([a-z0-9 .'-]+) in", 1, None),
            (r"the continent of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "country_of",
        [
            (r"which country has ([a-z0-9 .'-]+) as (?:its|the) capital", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 .'-]+) is the capital of which country", 1, None),
            (r"which country is ([a-z0-9 .'-]+) located in", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 .'-]+) is located in which country", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="capital_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "symbol_of",
        [
            (r"what is the chemical symbol of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"chemical symbol for ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"what is the symbol of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "element_with_symbol",
        [
            (r"which element has the symbol ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"symbol ([a-z0-9 .'-]+) belongs to which element", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="symbol_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "number_of",
        [
            (r"what is the atomic number of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"atomic number of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "element_with_number",
        [
            (r"which element has the atomic number ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="number_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "category_of",
        [
            (r"what category of element is ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"category of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "capital_of_country",
        [
            (r"what is the capital of the country of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"capital of the country of ([a-z0-9 .'-]+)", 1, None),

        chain=("country_of", "capital_of"),
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "continent_of_country",

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Fact builders
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def build_forward_facts_domain_a() -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    facts = []
    for line in COUNTRIES.strip().splitlines():
        c, cap, cont = [x.strip() for x in line.split("|")]
        facts.append((c, "capital_of", cap))
        facts.append((c, "continent_of", cont))

    for line in ELEMENTS.strip().splitlines():
        sym, name, num, cat = [x.strip() for x in line.split("|")]
        facts.append((name, "symbol_of", sym))
        facts.append((name, "number_of", num))
        facts.append((name, "category_of", cat))

    return facts

def build_domain_b_facts() -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    facts = []
    for c, cap, cont in B_COUNTRIES:
        facts.append((c, "capital_of", cap))
        facts.append((c, "continent_of", cont))
    return facts

def domain_b_gen_rules():
    return {
        "country_of": [
            {
                "kind": "index_range",
                "quot": B_SPAN,
                "table": [c for c, _, _ in B_COUNTRIES],
            }
        ]
    }

def domain_b_gen_entities():
    return [{"rx": r"\bp(\d{4})\b", "fmt": "P{:04d}"}]

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Query builders
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def domain_a_queries() -> List[Tuple[str, str, Optional[str]]]:
    q = []

    countries = [tuple(x.strip() for x in line.split("|")) for line in COUNTRIES.strip().splitlines()]
    elements = [tuple(x.strip() for x in line.split("|")) for line in ELEMENTS.strip().splitlines()]

    for c, cap, cont in countries:
        q.append(("direct", f"what is the capital of {c}?", cap))
        q.append(("direct_continent", f"what continent is {c} in?", cont))
        q.append(("reverse", f"which country has {cap} as its capital?", c))
        q.append(("chain", f"what is the capital of the country of {cap}?", cap))
        q.append(("distractor", f"i visited {cont} last year. anyway, what is the capital of {c}?", cap))

    for alias, canon in ALIASES.items():
        for c, cap, cont in countries:
            if c == canon:
                q.append(("alias", f"what is the capital of {alias}?", cap))
                break

    for clue, canon in CLUES.items():
        for c, cap, cont in countries:
            if c == canon:
                q.append(("clue", f"what is the capital of the {clue} country?", cap))
                break

    for sym, name, num, cat in elements:
        q.append(("direct", f"what is the chemical symbol of {name}?", sym))
        q.append(("direct", f"what is the atomic number of {name}?", num))
        q.append(("direct", f"what category of element is {name}?", cat))
        q.append(("reverse", f"which element has the symbol {sym}?", name))
        q.append(("reverse", f"which element has the atomic number {num}?", name))

    for f in FICTION:
        q.append(("unknown", f"what is the capital of {f}?", None))
        q.append(("unknown", f"what is the chemical symbol of {f}?", None))

    return q

def domain_b_queries(sample: int = 120) -> List[Tuple[str, str, Optional[str]]]:
    q = []
    step = max(1, B_N_PROV // sample)
    for i in range(0, B_N_PROV, step):
        country, capital, cont = B_COUNTRIES[i // B_SPAN]
        p = f"P{i:04d}"
        q.append(("direct", f"which country is {p} located in?", country))
        q.append(("chain", f"what is the capital of the country of {p}?", capital))
        q.append(("direct_continent", f"what continent is the country of {p} in?", cont))
    return q

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Evaluation
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def evaluate(dna: KnowledgeDNA, queries: List[Tuple[str, str, Optional[str]]]) -> dict:
    per = {}
    tuples = set()
    correct = 0
    total = 0
    false_accepts = 0
    unknown_total = 0

    for cat, prompt, expected in queries:
        total += 1
        parsed = dna.encode_query(prompt)
        ret = dna.retrieve(parsed)
        _ = dna.decode(ret)

        d = per.setdefault(cat, {"n": 0, "ok": 0, "answered": 0})
        d["n"] += 1

        if expected is None:
            unknown_total += 1
            answered = ret.answer is not None
            d["answered"] += int(answered)
            if answered:
                false_accepts += 1
            good = not answered
        else:
            answered = ret.answer is not None
            d["answered"] += int(answered)
            good = answered and norm(ret.answer) == norm(expected)

        if good:
            correct += 1
            for t in ret.tuples:
                tuples.add(t)

    return {
        "per": per,
        "tuples": tuples,
        "correct": correct,
        "total": total,
        "accuracy": correct / max(1, total),
        "false_accepts": false_accepts,
        "unknown_total": unknown_total,
    }

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# MDL diagnostic: simple inverse candidate report
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def auto_inverse_candidates(atoms: List[Tuple[str, str, str]], threshold: float = 0.8):
    by_rel = defaultdict(set)
    for s, r, o in atoms:
        by_rel[r].add((s, o))

    candidates = []
    rels = list(by_rel.keys())
    for r1 in rels:
        set1 = by_rel[r1]
        if not set1:
            continue
        for r2 in rels:
            if r1 == r2:
                continue
            # r2 is inverse of r1 if (o, r2, s) exists for many (s, r1, o)
            inv_set = {(o, s) for s, o in by_rel[r2]}
            overlap = len(set1 & inv_set)
            score = overlap / max(1, len(set1))
            if score >= threshold:
                candidates.append((r1, r2, score, overlap))
    return sorted(candidates, key=lambda x: -x[2])

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Reporting
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def print_category_table(per: dict):
    print(f"{'Category':<22}{'N':<8}{'Accuracy':<12}{'Answered'}")
    print("-" * 60)
    for cat, d in sorted(per.items()):
        acc = 100.0 * d["ok"] / max(1, d["n"])
        print(f"{cat:<22}{d['n']:<8}{acc:<12.1f}{d['answered']}")

def run_domain(name: str, facts: List[Tuple[str, str, str]], queries, aliases=None, clues=None,
               gen_rules=None, gen_entities=None, n_slots=4096, theoretical_tuples: Optional[int] = None):
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print(f"🧪 {name}")
    print("=" * 100)

    dna = KnowledgeDNA(n_slots=n_slots)
    dna.compile_from_teacher(
        facts,
        aliases=aliases or {},
        clues=clues or {},
        rels=REL_SPECS,
        gen_rules=gen_rules or {},
        gen_entities=gen_entities or [],
        n_slots=n_slots,
    )

    stored_atoms = dna.n_facts()
    res = evaluate(dna, queries)
    size = dna.size_bytes()

    observed_tuples = len(res["tuples"])
    if theoretical_tuples is not None:
        d_derive = theoretical_tuples / max(1, stored_atoms)
        tuple_note = f"observed sampled tuples={observed_tuples}, theoretical answerable tuples={theoretical_tuples}"
    else:
        d_derive = observed_tuples / max(1, stored_atoms)
        tuple_note = f"observed answerable tuples={observed_tuples}"

    print(f"Stored atoms              : {stored_atoms}")
    print(f"{tuple_note}")
    print(f"D_derive                  : {d_derive:.2f}")
    print(f"Artifact size estimate    : {size:,} bytes = {size/1_000_000:.3f} MB")
    print(f"Queries                   : {res['total']}")
    print(f"Correct                   : {res['correct']}")
    print(f"Accuracy                  : {100.0 * res['accuracy']:.1f}%")
    print(f"Unknown false accepts     : {res['false_accepts']} / {res['unknown_total']}")
    print()
    print_category_table(res["per"])

    # MDL diagnostic
    cands = auto_inverse_candidates(facts, threshold=0.75)
    if cands:
        print("\nMDL inverse candidates (auto diagnostic):")
        for r1, r2, score, overlap in cands[:10]:
            print(f"  {r2} may be inverse of {r1} | overlap={overlap} | score={score:.2f}")

    return {
        "dna": dna,
        "stored_atoms": stored_atoms,
        "observed_tuples": observed_tuples,
        "theoretical_tuples": theoretical_tuples,
        "D_derive": d_derive,
        "size": size,
        "result": res,
    }

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Main experiment
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n📦 Building Domain A: fact-dense capitals + chemistry")
facts_a = build_forward_facts_domain_a()
queries_a = domain_a_queries()
out_a = run_domain(
    "DOMAIN A — Capitals + Chemistry (fact-dense)",
    facts_a,
    queries_a,
    aliases=ALIASES,
    clues=CLUES,
    n_slots=8192,
)

print("\n📦 Building Domain B: rule-dense procedural provinces")
facts_b = build_domain_b_facts()
queries_b = domain_b_queries(sample=120)
# theoretical: 4096 provinces * 3 derived claims + 32 stored atoms
theoretical_b = B_N_PROV * 3 + len(facts_b)
out_b = run_domain(
    "DOMAIN B — Procedural Provinces (rule-dense)",
    facts_b,
    queries_b,
    gen_rules=domain_b_gen_rules(),
    gen_entities=domain_b_gen_entities(),
    n_slots=2048,
    theoretical_tuples=theoretical_b,
)

# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n" + "=" * 100)
print("🏆 PD-6/HELIX MINI FINAL VERDICT")
print("=" * 100)

acc_a = out_a["result"]["accuracy"]
acc_b = out_b["result"]["accuracy"]

size_a = out_a["size"]

print(f"Domain B unknown false     : {fa_b}")
print(f"Domain B size              : {size_b:,} B")
print()

pass_a = acc_a >= 0.95 and fa_a == 0
pass_b = acc_b >= 0.95 and fa_b == 0 and D_b > 3.0

print("Decision gates:")
print(f"  Domain A reliability gate (acc>=95%, false_accept=0): {'✅ PASS' if pass_a else '🔴 FAIL'}")
print(f"  Domain B density gate (acc>=95%, false_accept=0, D_derive>3): {'✅ PASS' if pass_b else '🔴 FAIL'}")
print()

if pass_b:
    print("🎉 CORE RESULT:")

    print("   store compact rules/programs, not raw fact rows, to get high usable-knowledge density.")
else:
    print("⚠️ HONEST RESULT:")
    print("   D_derive > 3 not reached in this mini run. Need stronger MDL rule induction or more derivable domain.")

print()

import os
import re
import json
import math
import time
import hashlib
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional, List, Tuple, Dict, Iterable

import numpy as np

print("=" * 104)
print("🧬 PD-7: REAL TEACHER EXTRACTION BRIDGE — ONE CELL COLAB")
print("=" * 104)

t0 = time.time()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIG
# ------------------------------------------------------------
MODEL_NAME = "Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct"

# True = instant pipeline test using gold answers as fake teacher.
# False = real Qwen teacher extraction.
USE_MOCK_TEACHER = False

# Quick default. Increase for serious run.
MAX_EXTRACTION_PROMPTS_PER_RELATION = 2
MAX_NEW_TOKENS = 8

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. MINI HELIX ENGINE (inline, Colab-safe)
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def norm(s: str) -> str:
    return " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", str(s).lower()).split())

def entity_index(name: str) -> Optional[int]:
    m = re.search(r"(\d+)\s*$", str(name))
    return int(m.group(1)) if m else None

def h64(*parts) -> int:
    m = hashlib.blake2b(digest_size=8)
    for p in parts:
        if isinstance(p, str):
            p = p.encode("utf-8")
        elif isinstance(p, int):
            p = int(p).to_bytes(8, "little", signed=True)
        m.update(p)
        m.update(b"\x1f")
    return int.from_bytes(m.digest(), "little")

@dataclass
class RelSpec:
    name: str
    patterns: List[Tuple[str, int, Optional[int]]] = field(default_factory=list)
    inverse_of: Optional[str] = None
    chain: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]] = None

@dataclass
class Parsed:
    subject: Optional[str]
    relation: Optional[str]
    confidence: float = 0.0
    raw: str = ""

@dataclass
class Retrieval:
    answer: Optional[str]
    subject: Optional[str]
    relation: Optional[str]
    confidence: float
    kind: str
    trace: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    tuples: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)

class Vocab:
    def __init__(self):
        self.names: List[str] = []
        self.ids: Dict[str, int] = {}

    def id(self, name: str) -> int:
        name = str(name)
        i = self.ids.get(name)
        if i is None:
            i = len(self.names)
            self.ids[name] = i
            self.names.append(name)
        return i

    def get(self, i: int) -> str:
        return self.names[i]

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.names)

class WaveLattice:
    def __init__(self, n_slots: int = 4096, max_facts_per_cell: int = 32):
        self.n_slots = n_slots
        self.max_facts_per_cell = max_facts_per_cell
        self.slots: Dict[int, dict] = {}

    def _slot_id(self, ent_id: int, level: int) -> int:
        return h64("slot", ent_id, level) % self.n_slots

    def write(self, ent_id: int, rel_id: int, obj_ref: int) -> None:
        for lvl in range(8):
            sid = self._slot_id(ent_id, lvl)
            cell = self.slots.get(sid)

            if cell is None:
                self.slots[sid] = {
                    "ent": ent_id,
                    "level": lvl,
                    "facts": [(rel_id, obj_ref)]
                }
                return

            if cell["ent"] == ent_id and cell["level"] == lvl:
                if (rel_id, obj_ref) in cell["facts"]:
                    return
                if len(cell["facts"]) < self.max_facts_per_cell:
                    cell["facts"].append((rel_id, obj_ref))
                    return
                continue

            # foreign collision -> spiral next level

        raise RuntimeError("Spiral level overflow")

    def read(self, ent_id: int, rel_id: int) -> Optional[int]:
        for lvl in range(8):
            sid = self._slot_id(ent_id, lvl)
            cell = self.slots.get(sid)
            if cell is None:
                continue
            if cell["ent"] != ent_id or cell["level"] != lvl:
                continue
            for r, o in cell["facts"]:
                if r == rel_id:
                    return o
        return None

    def iter_facts(self) -> Iterable[Tuple[int, int, int]]:
        for cell in self.slots.values():
            ent = cell["ent"]
            for r, o in cell["facts"]:
                yield ent, r, o

    def n_facts(self) -> int:
        return sum(len(c["facts"]) for c in self.slots.values())

    def size_estimate(self) -> int:
        return sum(16 + 3 * len(c["facts"]) for c in self.slots.values())

class RuleEngine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.rels: Dict[str, RelSpec] = {}
        self.aliases: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.clues: Dict[str, str] = {}

    def add_rel(self, spec: RelSpec):
        self.rels[spec.name] = spec

    def canon(self, name: str) -> str:
        return self.aliases.get(str(name).lower(), name)

class KnowledgeDNA:
    def __init__(self, n_slots: int = 4096):
        self.entities = Vocab()
        self.rules = RuleEngine()
        self.lattice = WaveLattice(n_slots=n_slots)
        self.rel_ids: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self.rel_names: Dict[int, str] = {}
        self.reverse_index: Dict[Tuple[int, int], int] = {}

    def compile_from_teacher(
        self,
        facts: Iterable[Tuple[str, str, str]],
        aliases: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        clues: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        rels: Optional[List[RelSpec]] = None,
        n_slots: int = 4096,
    ):
        self.lattice = WaveLattice(n_slots=n_slots)
        self.entities = Vocab()
        self.rules = RuleEngine()
        self.rel_ids = {}
        self.rel_names = {}
        self.reverse_index = {}

        if rels:
            for r in rels:
                self.rules.add_rel(r)

        if aliases:
            for k, v in aliases.items():
                self.rules.aliases[str(k).lower()] = v

        if clues:
            for k, v in clues.items():
                self.rules.clues[str(k).lower()] = v

        prepared = []
        for s, r, o in facts:
            s = self.rules.canon(s)
            o = self.rules.canon(o)
            if r not in self.rel_ids:
                self.rel_ids[r] = len(self.rel_ids)
                self.rel_names[self.rel_ids[r]] = r
            prepared.append((s, self.rel_ids[r], o))

        for s, rid, o in prepared:
            ent = self.entities.id(s)
            obj = self.entities.id(o)
            self.lattice.write(ent, rid, obj)

        for ent, rid, obj in self.lattice.iter_facts():
            self.reverse_index.setdefault((rid, obj), ent)

        return self

    def _entity_vocab(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        v = {n.lower(): n for n in self.entities.names}
        for a, c in self.rules.aliases.items():
            v.setdefault(a.lower(), c)
        for c, n in self.rules.clues.items():
            v.setdefault(c.lower(), n)
        return v

    def _link_entities(self, text: str) -> List[Tuple[int, int, str]]:
        low = text.lower()
        vocab = self._entity_vocab()
        hits = []

        for surface, canon in vocab.items():
            if not surface:
                continue
            pat = r"(?<![a-z0-9])" + re.escape(surface) + r"(?![a-z0-9])"
            for m in re.finditer(pat, low):
                hits.append((m.start(), m.end(), canon))

        hits.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], -(t[1] - t[0])))
        chosen = []
        last_end = -1
        for i, j, c in hits:
            if i >= last_end:
                chosen.append((i, j, c))
                last_end = j
        return chosen

    @staticmethod
    def _pick_subject(mentions, m, group: int, text: str) -> Optional[str]:
        if not mentions:
            return None
        try:
            span = m.group(group)
        except Exception:
            span = None

        if span:
            inside = [c for i, j, c in mentions if m.start() <= i and j <= m.end()]
            if inside:
                return inside[0]

        mid = (m.start() + m.end()) / 2.0
        return min(mentions, key=lambda t: abs((t[0] + t[1]) / 2.0 - mid))[2]

    def encode_query(self, text: str) -> Parsed:
        low = text.lower()
        mentions = self._link_entities(text)
        best = None
        best_score = (-1, -1, -1)

        for name, spec in self.rules.rels.items():
            for pat, sgrp, _ in spec.patterns:
                m = re.search(pat, low)
                if not m:
                    continue

                is_chain = 1 if spec.chain else 0
                is_inv = 1 if spec.inverse_of else 0
                score = (is_chain, is_inv, len(m.group(0)))

                if score <= best_score:
                    continue

                subj = self._pick_subject(mentions, m, sgrp, text)
                if subj is None:
                    continue

                best = Parsed(subject=subj, relation=name, confidence=0.95, raw=text)
                best_score = score

        return best if best is not None else Parsed(None, None, 0.0, text)

    def _rel_id(self, name: str) -> Optional[int]:
        return self.rel_ids.get(name)

    def _reverse_lookup(self, rel_id: int, obj_ref: int) -> Optional[int]:
        return self.reverse_index.get((rel_id, obj_ref))

    def _read(self, ent_name: str, rel_name: str):
        rid = self._rel_id(rel_name)
        eid = self.entities.ids.get(ent_name)

        if eid is None or rid is None:
            return None

        obj = self.lattice.read(eid, rid)
        if obj is None:
            return None

        ans = self.entities.get(obj)
        return ans, 1.00, [(ent_name, rel_name, ans)]

    def _read_or_derive(self, ent_name: str, rel_name: str):
        got = self._read(ent_name, rel_name)
        if got is not None:
            return got

        spec = self.rules.rels.get(rel_name)
        if spec and spec.inverse_of:
            base = spec.inverse_of
            rid = self._rel_id(base)
            eid = self.entities.ids.get(ent_name)
            if rid is not None and eid is not None:
                sub = self._reverse_lookup(rid, eid)
                if sub is not None:
                    ans = self.entities.get(sub)
                    return ans, 0.80, [(ans, base, ent_name), (ent_name, rel_name, ans)]
        return None

    def retrieve(self, parsed: Parsed) -> Retrieval:
        if not parsed.subject or not parsed.relation:
            return Retrieval(None, parsed.subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", ["parse_fail"], [])

        subject = self.rules.canon(parsed.subject)
        spec = self.rules.rels.get(parsed.relation)

        if spec and spec.chain:
            cur = subject
            tuples = []
            trace = [f"chain:{parsed.relation}"]
            for step in spec.chain:
                res = self._read_or_derive(cur, step)
                if res is None:
                    return Retrieval(None, subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", trace + [f"miss:{step}"], [])
                ans, score, tups = res
                trace.append(f"{cur} -[{step}]-> {ans}")
                tuples.extend(tups)
                cur = ans
            tuples.append((subject, parsed.relation, cur))
            return Retrieval(cur, subject, parsed.relation, 0.90, "derived", trace, tuples)

        res = self._read_or_derive(subject, parsed.relation)
        if res is not None:
            ans, score, tuples = res
            kind = "fact" if score >= 0.99 else "derived"
            return Retrieval(ans, subject, parsed.relation, score, kind, [f"{kind}:{subject}"], tuples)

        return Retrieval(None, subject, parsed.relation, 0.0, "unknown", ["miss"], [])

    def decode(self, ret: Retrieval) -> str:
        if ret.answer is None:
            return "I don't know."
        templates = [
            "{a}",
            "The answer is {a}.",
            "It is {a}.",
            "According to memory: {a}.",
        ]
        h = int(hashlib.md5(str(ret.answer).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(), 16)
        return templates[h % len(templates)].format(a=ret.answer)

    def n_facts(self) -> int:
        return self.lattice.n_facts()

    def size_bytes(self) -> int:
        grammar_obj = {
            k: {
                "patterns": v.patterns,
                "inverse_of": v.inverse_of,
                "chain": list(v.chain) if v.chain else None,
            }
            for k, v in self.rules.rels.items()
        }
        lexicon_obj = {
            "aliases": self.rules.aliases,
            "clues": self.rules.clues,
        }
        namespace_obj = {"entities": self.entities.names}

        grammar = len(json.dumps(grammar_obj, sort_keys=True))
        lexicon = len(json.dumps(lexicon_obj, sort_keys=True))
        namespace = len(json.dumps(namespace_obj, sort_keys=True))
        lattice = self.lattice.size_estimate()
        return 64 + grammar + lexicon + namespace + lattice

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. RELATION SPECS (hand-written rule algebra)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
REL_SPECS = [
    RelSpec(
        "capital_of",
        [
            (r"(?:what is |what's |whats )?the capital (?:city )?of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 '-]+)'s capital (?:city )?is", 1, None),
            (r"capital of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "continent_of",
        [
            (r"what continent is ([a-z0-9 '-]+) in", 1, None),
            (r"the continent of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "country_of",
        [
            (r"(?:which|what) country has ([a-z0-9 '-]+) as (?:its|the) capital", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 '-]+) is the capital of which country", 1, None),
            (r"(?:which|what) country is ([a-z0-9 '-]+) located in", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 '-]+) is located in which country", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="capital_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "symbol_of",
        [
            (r"(?:what is |what's )?the (?:chemical )?symbol (?:of|for) ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"chemical symbol for ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"state the symbol for ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "element_with_symbol",
        [
            (r"(?:which|what) element has (?:the )?symbol ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"symbol ([a-z0-9 '-]+) belongs to which element", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="symbol_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "number_of",
        [
            (r"atomic number of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"what is the atomic number of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 '-]+) has atomic number", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "element_with_number",
        [
            (r"(?:which|what) element has (?:the )?atomic number ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
        inverse_of="number_of",
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "category_of",
        [
            (r"what (?:type|kind|category) of element is ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"category of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"([a-z0-9 '-]+) is a", 1, None),
        ],
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "capital_of_country",
        [
            (r"what is the capital of the country of ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
            (r"capital of the country (?:containing|of) ([a-z0-9 '-]+)", 1, None),
        ],
        chain=("country_of", "capital_of"),
    ),
    RelSpec(
        "continent_of_country",
        [
            (r"what continent is the country of ([a-z0-9 '-]+) in", 1, None),
        ],
        chain=("country_of", "continent_of"),
    ),
]

    if "clue" in c:
        CLUES[c["clue"].lower()] = c["name"]

    "What is the capital of Atlantis?",
    "What is the capital of Nowhereland?",
    "What is the chemical symbol for Unobtainium?",
    "What is the chemical symbol for Fictionium?",
    "What is the capital of Moonville?",
    "Which element has the atomic number 999?",
