# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. GOLD FACTS (for comparison DNA)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def build_gold_facts() -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    facts = []
    for c in CONCEPTS:
        if c["kind"] == "country":
            facts.append((c["name"], "capital_of", c["answer"]))
            facts.append((c["name"], "continent_of", c["continent"]))
        else:
            facts.append((c["name"], "symbol_of", c["answer"]))
            facts.append((c["name"], "number_of", c["number"]))
            facts.append((c["name"], "category_of", c["category"]))
    return facts

GOLD_FACTS = build_gold_facts()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. EXTRACTION PROMPTS (teacher sees these)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
EXTRACTION_TEMPLATES = {
    "country": {
        "capital_of": [
            "The capital of {name} is",
            "{name}'s capital is",
            "Please name the capital of {name}:",
        ],
        "continent_of": [
            "{name} is located in the continent of",
            "The continent of {name} is",
        ],
    },
    "chemical": {
        "symbol_of": [
            "The chemical symbol for {name} is",
            "{name} has the chemical symbol",
        ],
        "number_of": [
            "The atomic number of {name} is",
            "{name} has atomic number",
        ],
        "category_of": [
            "{name} is a",
            "The category of element {name} is",
        ],
    },
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. EVAL PROMPTS (DNA-only student sees these)
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def build_eval_queries() -> List[Tuple[str, str, Optional[str]]]:
    qs = []

    for c in CONCEPTS:
        name = c["name"]
        kind = c["kind"]

        if kind == "country":
            cap = c["answer"]
            cont = c["continent"]
            alias = c.get("alias")
            clue = c.get("clue")

            qs.append(("direct", f"What is the capital of {name}?", cap))
            qs.append(("direct_continent", f"What continent is {name} in?", cont))
            qs.append(("reverse", f"Which country has {cap} as its capital?", name))
            qs.append(("chain", f"What is the capital of the country of {cap}?", cap))
            qs.append(("chain_continent", f"What continent is the country of {cap} in?", cont))
            qs.append(("distractor", f"I visited {cont} last year. Anyway, what is the capital of {name}?", cap))

            if alias:
                qs.append(("alias", f"What is the capital of {alias}?", cap))
            if clue:
                qs.append(("clue", f"What is the capital of the {clue} country?", cap))

        else:
            sym = c["answer"]
            num = c["number"]
            cat = c["category"]
            alias = c.get("alias")

            qs.append(("direct", f"What is the chemical symbol of {name}?", sym))
            qs.append(("direct", f"What is the atomic number of {name}?", num))
            qs.append(("direct", f"What category of element is {name}?", cat))
            qs.append(("reverse", f"Which element has the symbol {sym}?", name))
            qs.append(("reverse", f"Which element has the atomic number {num}?", name))
            qs.append(("distractor", f"Although platinum is Pt, the symbol for {name.lower()} is", sym))

            if alias:
                qs.append(("alias", f"State the symbol for {alias}.", sym))

    for p in UNKNOWN_PROMPTS:
        qs.append(("unknown", p, None))

    return qs

EVAL_QUERIES = build_eval_queries()

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. TEACHER
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def load_real_teacher():
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

    print(f"🔄 Loading {MODEL_NAME}...")
    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_NAME)
    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dtype = torch.float16 if device == "cuda" else torch.float32

    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
        MODEL_NAME,
        torch_dtype=dtype,
        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,
    )
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()

    print(f"✅ Teacher loaded on {device} | dtype={dtype}")
    return model, tokenizer, device

def teacher_generate_real(teacher, prompt: str, max_new_tokens: int = MAX_NEW_TOKENS) -> str:
    import torch
    model, tokenizer, device = teacher

    inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(device)
    with torch.no_grad():
        outputs = model.generate(
            **inputs,
            max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
            do_sample=False,
            pad_token_id=tokenizer.eos_token_id,
            eos_token_id=tokenizer.eos_token_id,
        )

    gen = outputs[0][inputs.input_ids.shape[1]:]
    text = tokenizer.decode(gen, skip_special_tokens=True)
    return text

def clean_answer(s: str) -> str:
    s = str(s).strip()
    s = s.split("\n")[0].strip()

    # remove quotes
    s = re.sub(r"^[\"'‘’“”]+|[\"'‘’“”]+$", "", s).strip()

    s = s.lower()

    # remove common lead-ins
    s = re.sub(r"^(the|a|an|it is|is|capital of|city of|element|metal)\s+", "", s).strip()

    # cut at comma/semicolon/parenthesis
    s = re.split(r"[,;(\[]", s)[0].strip()

    # strip trailing punctuation
    s = s.rstrip(".!?:-").strip()

    # normalize spaces
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s)

    return s

def extract_atoms_real(teacher) -> Dict[Tuple[str, str], str]:
    print("\n🔬 Extracting atoms from real teacher...")

    buckets = defaultdict(list)
    total = 0

    for c in CONCEPTS:
        kind = c["kind"]
        name = c["name"]
        for rel, templates in EXTRACTION_TEMPLATES[kind].items():
            for tmpl in templates[:MAX_EXTRACTION_PROMPTS_PER_RELATION]:
                prompt = tmpl.format(name=name)
                raw = teacher_generate_real(teacher, prompt)
                clean = clean_answer(raw)
                if clean:
                    buckets[(name, rel)].append(clean)
                total += 1

                if total % 20 == 0:
                    print(f"   generated {total} prompts...")

    atoms = {}
    dropped = 0

    for key, answers in buckets.items():
        if not answers:
            dropped += 1
            continue

        cnt = Counter(answers)
        modal, n = cnt.most_common(1)[0]

        # For prototype, take modal even if weak. In production, mark low-confidence.
        atoms[key] = modal

    print(f"✅ Extracted {len(atoms)} atoms | dropped empty buckets: {dropped}")
    return atoms

def extract_atoms_mock() -> Dict[Tuple[str, str], str]:
    print("\n⚡ Using MOCK teacher (gold facts as perfect extraction)")
    return {(s, r): o for s, r, o in GOLD_FACTS}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. EVALUATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------
def evaluate_dna(dna: KnowledgeDNA, queries: List[Tuple[str, str, Optional[str]]]) -> Dict:
    per = {}
    tuples = set()
    correct = 0
    total = 0
    false_accepts = 0
    unknown_total = 0

    for cat, prompt, expected in queries:
        total += 1
        parsed = dna.encode_query(prompt)
        ret = dna.retrieve(parsed)
        _ = dna.decode(ret)

        d = per.setdefault(cat, {"n": 0, "ok": 0, "answered": 0})
        d["n"] += 1

        if expected is None:
            unknown_total += 1
            answered = ret.answer is not None
            d["answered"] += int(answered)
            good = not answered
            if good:
                d["ok"] += 1
                correct += 1
            if answered:
                false_accepts += 1
        else:
            answered = ret.answer is not None
            d["answered"] += int(answered)
            good = answered and norm(ret.answer) == norm(expected)
            if good:
                d["ok"] += 1
                correct += 1
                for t in ret.tuples:
                    tuples.add(t)

    return {
        "per": per,
        "tuples": tuples,
        "correct": correct,
        "total": total,
        "accuracy": correct / max(1, total),
        "false_accepts": false_accepts,
        "unknown_total": unknown_total,
    }

def print_category_table(per: dict):
    print(f"{'Category':<22}{'N':<8}{'Accuracy':<12}{'Answered'}")
    print("-" * 62)
    for cat, d in sorted(per.items()):
        acc = 100.0 * d["ok"] / max(1, d["n"])
        print(f"{cat:<22}{d['n']:<8}{acc:<12.1f}{d['answered']}")

def compile_dna_from_atoms(atoms: Dict[Tuple[str, str], str]) -> KnowledgeDNA:
    facts = [(s, r, o) for (s, r), o in atoms.items()]
    dna = KnowledgeDNA(n_slots=4096)
    dna.compile_from_teacher(
        facts,
        aliases=ALIASES,
        clues=CLUES,
        rels=REL_SPECS,
        n_slots=4096,
    )
    return dna

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. MAIN BRIDGE EXPERIMENT
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n📦 Gold-table facts:", len(GOLD_FACTS))
print("📋 Eval queries:", len(EVAL_QUERIES))

# Gold DNA baseline
gold_atoms = {(s, r): o for s, r, o in GOLD_FACTS}
dna_gold = compile_dna_from_atoms(gold_atoms)
res_gold = evaluate_dna(dna_gold, EVAL_QUERIES)

print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 GOLD-TABLE DNA BASELINE")
print("=" * 104)
print(f"Stored atoms              : {dna_gold.n_facts()}")
print(f"Answerable tuples         : {len(res_gold['tuples'])}")
print(f"D_derive                  : {len(res_gold['tuples']) / max(1, dna_gold.n_facts()):.2f}")
print(f"Artifact size estimate    : {dna_gold.size_bytes():,} bytes")
print(f"Queries                   : {res_gold['total']}")
print(f"Correct                   : {res_gold['correct']}")
print(f"Accuracy                  : {100.0 * res_gold['accuracy']:.1f}%")
print(f"Unknown false accepts     : {res_gold['false_accepts']} / {res_gold['unknown_total']}")
print()
print_category_table(res_gold["per"])

# Teacher extraction
if USE_MOCK_TEACHER:
    teacher_atoms = extract_atoms_mock()
    teacher = None
else:
    teacher = load_real_teacher()
    teacher_atoms = extract_atoms_real(teacher)

# Delete/free teacher before DNA-only eval
if not USE_MOCK_TEACHER and teacher is not None:
    print("\n🧹 Deleting/freeing teacher before DNA-only evaluation...")
    try:
        import torch
        del teacher[0]
        del teacher[1]
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    except Exception:
        pass

dna_teacher = compile_dna_from_atoms(teacher_atoms)
res_teacher = evaluate_dna(dna_teacher, EVAL_QUERIES)

print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🧪 REAL-TEACHER-EXTRACTED DNA")
print("=" * 104)
print(f"Stored atoms              : {dna_teacher.n_facts()}")
print(f"Answerable tuples         : {len(res_teacher['tuples'])}")
print(f"D_derive                  : {len(res_teacher['tuples']) / max(1, dna_teacher.n_facts()):.2f}")
print(f"Artifact size estimate    : {dna_teacher.size_bytes():,} bytes")
print(f"Queries                   : {res_teacher['total']}")
print(f"Correct                   : {res_teacher['correct']}")
print(f"Accuracy                  : {100.0 * res_teacher['accuracy']:.1f}%")
print(f"Unknown false accepts     : {res_teacher['false_accepts']} / {res_teacher['unknown_total']}")
print()
print_category_table(res_teacher["per"])

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. EXTRACTION LOSS ANALYSIS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("📊 EXTRACTION LOSS: GOLD DNA VS TEACHER DNA")
print("=" * 104)

gold_acc = res_gold["accuracy"]
teacher_acc = res_teacher["accuracy"]
gap = gold_acc - teacher_acc

print(f"Gold-table DNA accuracy     : {100.0 * gold_acc:.1f}%")
print(f"Teacher-extracted DNA acc   : {100.0 * teacher_acc:.1f}%")
print(f"Extraction loss gap         : {100.0 * gap:.1f} percentage points")
print()

# Show atoms where teacher differed from gold
print("Atom-level mismatches (teacher vs gold):")
mismatch_count = 0
for (s, r), gold_o in gold_atoms.items():
    t_o = teacher_atoms.get((s, r))
    if t_o is None:
        print(f"  MISSING  {s} | {r} | gold={gold_o}")
        mismatch_count += 1
    elif norm(t_o) != norm(gold_o):
        print(f"  WRONG    {s} | {r} | gold={gold_o} | teacher={t_o}")
        mismatch_count += 1

if mismatch_count == 0:
    print("  none — teacher extraction matched gold table")
else:
    print(f"\nTotal atom mismatches: {mismatch_count} / {len(gold_atoms)}")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. SAMPLE WRONG/UNKNOWN ANSWERS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print(" SAMPLE FAILURES — TEACHER DNA")
print("=" * 104)

failures = []
for cat, prompt, expected in EVAL_QUERIES:
    parsed = dna_teacher.encode_query(prompt)
    ret = dna_teacher.retrieve(parsed)
    out = dna_teacher.decode(ret)

    if expected is None:
        if ret.answer is not None:
            failures.append((cat, prompt, "UNKNOWN_EXPECTED", ret.answer, out))
    else:
        if ret.answer is None or norm(ret.answer) != norm(expected):
            failures.append((cat, prompt, expected, ret.answer, out))

if not failures:
    print("No failures on eval set.")
else:
    print(f"{'Type':<18}{'Prompt':<58}{'Expected':<18}{'Got':<18}")
    print("-" * 112)
    for typ, prompt, exp, got, out in failures[:30]:
        short_p = prompt if len(prompt) <= 56 else prompt[:53] + "..."
        exp_s = str(exp) if exp is not None else "None"
        got_s = str(got) if got is not None else "None"
        print(f"{typ:<18}{short_p:<58}{exp_s:<18}{got_s:<18}")

    if len(failures) > 30:
        print(f"... and {len(failures)-30} more failures")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. DECISION GATE
# ------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n" + "=" * 104)
print("🏆 PD-7 DECISION GATE")
print("=" * 104)

gate_gold_ok = gold_acc >= 0.98
gate_teacher_ok = teacher_acc >= 0.90
gate_gap_ok = gap <= 0.08
gate_unknown_ok = res_teacher["false_accepts"] == 0
gate_size_ok = dna_teacher.size_bytes() <= 100_000

print(f"1. Gold DNA accuracy >= 98%        : {'✅' if gate_gold_ok else '🔴'} ({100.0*gold_acc:.1f}%)")
print(f"2. Teacher DNA accuracy >= 90%     : {'✅' if gate_teacher_ok else '🔴'} ({100.0*teacher_acc:.1f}%)")
print(f"3. Extraction gap <= 8pp           : {'✅' if gate_gap_ok else '🔴'} ({100.0*gap:.1f}pp)")
print(f"4. Unknown false accepts == 0      : {'✅' if gate_unknown_ok else '🔴'} ({res_teacher['false_accepts']})")
print(f"5. Teacher DNA size <= 100KB       : {'✅' if gate_size_ok else '🔴'} ({dna_teacher.size_bytes():,} B)")
print()

all_pass = gate_gold_ok and gate_teacher_ok and gate_gap_ok and gate_unknown_ok and gate_size_ok

if all_pass:
    print("🎉 BRIDGE PASSED:")
    print("   Real teacher knowledge can be extracted into HELIX DNA atoms")
    print("   and the DNA-only student recovers gold-level behavior on held-out paraphrases.")
    print("   Next: scale concepts, add MDL rule induction, interference injection.")
else:
    print("⚠️ BRIDGE NOT FULLY PASSED:")
    print("   This is expected on first small real-teacher run.")
    print("   Diagnose from atom mismatches and failure samples above.")
    print("   Next fixes:")
    print("     - better answer cleaning/parsing")
    print("     - more extraction prompts per relation")
    print("     - relation-specific confidence thresholds")
    print("     - larger concept set")
    print("     - automatic inverse/generative rule induction")

print()
print("IMPORTANT HONEST LIMIT:")
print("  PD-7 tests narrow-domain stored knowledge extraction.")
print("  It does NOT prove full general LLM replacement.")
print("  It does NOT prove 1MB stores arbitrary trillion-token knowledge.")

print(f"\n⏱️ Total time: {time.time() - t0:.1f}s")
